- **2026-10-30: Faster Heating is 10 / 7 / 5 / 4 s** (Allen: "3 seconds seems a bit quick"). Supersedes 10 / 6 / 4 / 3 (2026-10-27). [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, evening list]]
- **2026-10-30: Story letters count among More Clients' letters at once** (Allen: "My upgrades says i should only have 2 letters at once, but i got more offers than that right now"). [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, evening list]]
- **2026-10-30: The anvil's bar marks where Finish opens; the hilt reveal holds 0.35 s; polishing scrapes** (Allen: "a notch or mark once it hit 9/12"; "it just stays still for too long"; "A polishing sound while its happening would be nice"). [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, evening list]]
- **2026-10-30: Bag cards get a trash and a − N + count; the crew window shows locked crew and its button is always there; letters' wanted swords are bold red, with a quest tracker under the Mail button** (Allen's words in the session note). On a stack the count shows after the first trash tap (an always-on one doesn't fit a phone's card). [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, evening list]]
- **2026-10-30: Decorate is hidden for a later release** (Allen: "Hide the decorate feature for later release"): its controller and button are disabled, kept. [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, evening list]]
- **2026-10-30: Only the 3 cheapest pads stand on the floor, plus the next sword and the Delivery Cart** (Allen: "Go thru with the upgrades pads", after [[Design/Refinements Proposals — 2026-10-30]]'s option A). A pad that isn't standing can't be bought. Simulated: nobody slower. [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, Allen's answers (evening)]]
- **2026-10-30: The furnace starts at 15 s; Faster Heating 15 / 11 / 8 / 6** (Allen: "Set the forge to start at 15 seconds. And work the faster upgrades from there"). Supersedes the 10 / 7 / 5 / 4 of the same evening. [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, Allen's answers (evening)]]
- **2026-10-30: Crew levels can be bought with gems: 20 / 55 / 105 / 200 for Lv. 2–5** (Allen: "Crew levels increase those gems cost by 1/3", of the proposed 15 / 40 / 80 / 150). The level's work is credited; "Swords Made" stays true. [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, Allen's answers (evening)]]
- **2026-10-30: The anvil takes 10 hits, not 12** (Allen: "Yes do 10 hits"); Finish opens at 8 of 10 (the 75% share rounded up; an early finish grades 80%, was 75%). [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, Allen's answers (evening)]]
- **2026-10-30: The Sword Crafter's pad waits under the pad cap like the others** (Allen: "Keep the cap", asked whether it should always stand like the next sword). [[Sessions/2026-10-30#Refinements, Allen's answers (evening)]]
- **2026-10-31: The mail tracker is a bullet list** (Allen: "takes upp too much width when it doesnt need to. remove the background on the close button. make it look more like a bullet point list"): no pills; each row is a "•" and its title, as wide as the text; the fold chevron without its circle. [[Sessions/2026-10-31#The mail tracker as a bullet list]]
- **2026-10-31: A rolled ore an open letter or order needs says who wants it** (Allen: "if a player rolls an ore thats required to use in the quest, theres another label shown on it"): "<sender> wants this!" under the rarity for mail orders too (it was only the story letters'), from the same check as Auto-Roll's quest stop. Its light text shows again (hidden since 2026-10-30). [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Quest ores say who wants them]]
- **2026-10-31: An Auto Anvil pass, 49 R$, strikes the anvil for you** (Allen: "auto-anvil clicker that can be super cheap?"). A switch at the top of your anvil's view, shown once the lessons are done; with it on, each blow lands at your Faster Strikes pace, the same as a tap. The hilt seat stays by hand. Price Claude's: half the cheapest pass (Swift Boots, 99). [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Auto Anvil]]
- **2026-10-31: Your crew show their level in the world, and you can level them up there with gems** (Allen: "add an in-game progress bar or model for the employees, that show how far it is to upgrade ... maybe just walking near your employee shows the text or prompt"). A bar under "Lv. N" on your own crew only (gone at Lv. 5); the nearest worker within 8 studs gets a level-up prompt (F / gamepad Y), giving way to station prompts; a level gained plays the pad-buy burst. Claude's defaults. [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Crew levels in the world (subagent)]]
- **2026-10-31: Crew level-ups are a tap, then a small confirm window** (Allen: "instead of having hold to upgrade the employee, it should be a tap, and then another small window pops up asking to confirm"). "<name> to Lv. N?" with the gem price on the green button; short of gems, Roblox's own Robux prompt opens and the window stays. Supersedes the same day's 0.6 s hold. The crew page's button reads "Lv. 4 → Lv. 5" over the gem icon and price (Allen: "lvl 4 -> lvl 5 and then 105 gems with the icon below"), and every level reads "Lv." (was "Lvl."). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — UI notes]]
- **2026-10-31: No custom pack popup: short of coins or gems goes straight to Roblox's purchase prompt** (Allen: "it should just pop upon the official roblox buy with robux. opening our custom one then to open another one is unneeded friction"). The smallest pack that covers the gap is still the one offered. Supersedes the popup card of 2026-10-28's "Short of coins or gems: offer the covering pack". Every pass and product now has an icon on Roblox from the icon pack (Allen: "update the developer products and gamepasses images"). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — UI notes]]
- **2026-10-31: Robux offers wait for the gem step, not just the letter** (the first-10-minutes review). Pass locks, the Auto-Roll bar, the Starter Pack dot, pack prompts, the world spots and the Auto Anvil switch wait until the tutorial **and** its gem buy are done. The Shop window's own Buy buttons stay, since the player opens it on purpose. Narrows 2026-10-28 Fixes2's `TutorialDone` for these offers. [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Robux offers wait for the gem step]]
- **2026-10-31: Three Robux passes stand in the world as objects** (Allen, 2026-10-30: "players are more likely to buy something they can see in the game rather than opening shop and clicking"; 2026-10-31: "more in-game ways to prompt players to buy things"). The Auto Ore Roller's motor by the lever, Lucky Ore's glass case by the stands (odds panel first, hidden where paid random items are restricted) and 50% More Coins' chest in the shop; each turns into its owned look once bought. Only on your plot after the lessons; visitors see only owned ones. Where Swift Boots, VIP and the Starter Pack go is open. [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Robux passes you can see in the world (subagent)]]
- **2026-10-31: The beginner shop has a lower roof, the upgraded shop a second roof tier** (Allen: "make the shop have a lower roof and the upgraded shop have another layer roof ... for thumbnails we can do a comparison"). Starter top 21.65 → 19.45 studs; the Upgraded Store gets a raised, louvred smoke-vent tier, top 38.45. The tier's size and louvres are open. [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Shop roofs: a low beginner shop, a two-tier upgraded one (subagent)]]
- **2026-10-31: Station views work on a controller and read better on a phone** (accessibility review, QoL note; Claude's defaults). Hold R2 to polish along the edge; pad glyphs on every station button; Auto Anvil on L1; bigger hint text; each blow rings a little higher and blow 10 hits harder. Pickers open on what you'd pick (the letter's blade, then your last-forged, then the dearest). Toasts last by their length. Roblox's thumbstick and jump button hide while a station view or window is up. Finish stays flat green (Allen's 2026-10-28 note), open. [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Station views: controllers, glyphs, readable text, comfort, pickers (subagent)]] · [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Station views hide Roblox's thumbstick]]
- **2026-10-31: Settings in three sections, with volume sliders and comfort switches** (Allen: "more granular settings controls"). SOUND: Music, Sound and a gear to a Volume window (Music, Effects, Alerts, 0–100% in steps of 10). SCREEN: Weather, Camera shake, Reduce motion (follows Roblox's own switch until the player picks), Vibration (touch or gamepad only). PLAY: Quest tracker (the tracker's fold, saved). Alerts (an offer, a ready furnace, the cart back) are their own group, so turning effects down never silences them. The cart's Pin is saved too. Supersedes 2026-10-23's "Music and Sound on/off" (picked then over volume sliders). [[Sessions/2026-10-31#Settings: sections, volumes, comfort switches, music (subagent)]]
- **2026-10-31: Music is three calm licensed tracks, looped from a random one, at 70%** (Claude's picks by name and length, not listened to: "Minimal Koto", "Kioku", "Aichaku"). Allen swaps any of them. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Settings]]
- **2026-10-31: A begun upgrade chain always stands; the pad cap only picks new chains** (Allen: "once an upgrade is available, it shouldn't then be locked behind something else. it should just be upgradable to the max if they have the coins"; "i bought everything in the base shop, and now theres nothing to buy?"). Once one upgrade of a chain is bought (or, for the Ore Slots, a merge is done), its next level is always on the floor. `PAD_CAP` 3 now only picks which new chains open. Chains: Ore Slots and Merge, Ingot Racks, Sword Racks, each furnace's Heating, Furnace 2 with its Heating, Faster Strikes, Polishing Station 2 with Polish Boost, the crafters (Anvil 2 → Anvil 4), the Etcher, the shop (Wall Rack 2 → … → Big Sword Stand), Rickshaw Bays. Supersedes the "next cheapest" part of 2026-10-30's "Only the 3 cheapest pads stand". About 9–10 pads now stand in the first hours (4–5 before): open whether to cap new chains at 1. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Upgrade progression]]
- **2026-10-31: Anvil 2 always stands once the base stations are bought** (Allen: "after getting all the base stations i should be able to buy employee, i shouldnt have to upgrade them fully"). Base stations are the Ingot Rack, Sword Rack and Furnace 2; Anvil 2 is in `PAD_ALWAYS` with the Delivery Cart, and the Sword Crafter follows in its chain. Supersedes 2026-10-30's "The Sword Crafter's pad waits under the pad cap like the others" (Allen: "Keep the cap"). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Upgrade progression]]
- **2026-10-31: Furnace 2 comes after Faster Heating I** (Allen: "i can buy a 2nd sword rack before i can upgrade my furnace again"; "i shouldnt have to upgrade them fully"). Supersedes Refinements 5's "Furnace 2 only after Faster Heating III" (2026-10-25), kept by the 2026-10-28 upgrade order. Open: should it need nothing at all? [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Upgrade progression]]
- **2026-10-31: No gate partway along a chain** (Allen, as above). Removed: Sword Rack 2's Katana, Anvil 4's Stocker, Rickshaw Bay 2's Longsword and Bay 3's Falchion, the Claymore's Rapier (it waits for the Zanbatō only), and the Sword Etcher's Gladius (its table already waits for it). Supersedes those stage gates from the 2026-10-28 upgrade order ("Two stages open"). The shop chain waits only on itself, so Display Table 5 never needs the Cashier. Gates stay only at a chain's start (Ingot Rack after Ore Slot 2, Faster Strikes I after the Wakizashi, Cashier after the Upgraded Store and Stocker, and so on). Simulated: the Sword Crafter 2–9 min later, the efficient Zanbatō 2:59 → 4:14 (open). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Upgrade progression]]
- **2026-10-31: Pads moved off busy spots; the pad base is charcoal** (the yard map review; Claude's spots). Ore Slots and Merge, each furnace's Heating, the Sword Crafter, the big swords and the Stocker each have a new spot; the pad base matches the hearths (58, 54, 54). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Upgrade progression]]
- **2026-10-31: A walk-in picks its sword inside, and only customers still shopping fill the shop** (Allen: "it feels like i only see 1 offer at a time in the store ... seems like if i place 2 swords on the shelf, then 2 customers walk in at most"). A customer chooses once inside; one with nothing free waits up to 8 s ("I'll wait my turn."), then leaves. A customer frees their place as their business ends, not when they reach the road. The rate (45–75 s, Better Advertising, weather, Promoter) and the cap (2 + Promoter + bays) are unchanged. Supersedes 2026-10-29's "they still take a place in the shop while there" for customers walking out. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Customers and values]]
- **2026-10-31: Walk-ins fade in and out; two customers at one holder stand side by side** (shop map review; Claude's defaults). Each rack and table slot has its own stand spot; the kanban name is GothamSSm Heavy with an outline. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Customers and values]]
- **2026-10-31: The anvil's shape chip shows what the forged sword sells for** (Allen: "at the forge it says 720 coins ... once im at the polishing station, the price is increased to 1018 base, why is that?"). The chip is the value with every strike, its hilt and the seat bonus, unpolished; it was the base price × metal only. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Customers and values]]
- **2026-10-31: "<sender> wants this!" follows the mail while the ore waits** (Allen: "not seeing the 'x wants this!' when i have a black iron quest"). Checked every second, so accepting an order after the roll shows the line. Supersedes the morning's "set when the ore lands". [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Customers and values]]
- **2026-10-31: Mail orders come every 5:00 at base, and never from the sender you just finished** (Allen: "I finished a mail request and got another one soon after from the same person. Lets also increase the base time between message requests to 5 minutes"). Quicker Mail's levels: 300 / 250 / 210 / 175 / 150 / 125 s. Supersedes 2026-10-28 More features' Quicker Mail 3:00 → 1:15. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: Walk-ins ramp up after the tutorial** (first-10-minutes review; Claude's default). The gap eases from 12–22 s to Better Advertising's over 5 minutes or 5 sales, whichever comes first; a returning player gets no ramp. Supersedes the tutorial pace stopping dead at `TutorialDone` (2026-10-27 customer rate, 2026-10-28 Fixes2). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The first sale says "Accept the offer!" once the card is up, and its customer never leaves unanswered** (Allen: "in tutorial when customer is coming, the tutorial message should say "Accept the offer!""). "A customer is coming!" points at the shelf until the card shows. No answer means a fresh 45 s card; a decline still sends them away. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The guide's arrows follow a walkable path** (Allen: "can we make tutorial arrow path finding ... it goes into the wall since its a beam"). A pathfinding route to a floor spot near the target that can see it; the straight line only while no path is ready. Supersedes straight arrows. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: No sword pads, no bag trash and 3 s heats until the tutorial letter is sent** (first-10-minutes review #7–#9). The Polishing Station is the tutorial's only buy. Heats stay 3 s until the letter is sent (was until your first sword, 2026-10-22). The bag's card and corner trash appear at Send; still no confirm. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The Katana letter waits for the Machete** (first-10-minutes review #6). It comes 45 s after the Machete is bought, with the 3 sales as before. Supersedes "after the wakizashi and the third shop sale" (2026-10-22). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The cheer sits at the top, and the daily window waits for it** (Allen: ""Create more swords to sell" message is off. it's in the middle. and there seems to be weird effects going on."). The banner takes the guide line's spot and its outline fades with it; the confetti stays (open). The lever line reads "Hold to pull the lever"; a Black Iron says "Black Iron isn't Iron. Pull again"; the next sword's pad label has a fill bar toward its price. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Tutorial fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The mail tracker reads in two lines on a phone** (Allen: "it should also read Black Iron / Wakizashi into two lines, instead of one long line"). The metal, then the sword, in fixed 18 pt text. Supersedes the morning's scaled text. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — UI notes]]
- **2026-10-31: A held roll button grows, fills a ring and rocks** (Allen: "holding the roll button with my finger tends to cover the actual button ... make it expand and maybe tween some type of rumble/rotate effect"). 1.5× with a gold ring filling over the hold; no rocking with Reduce motion. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — UI notes]]
- **2026-10-31: Auto-Roll's settings are a small dropdown under its bar** (Allen: "an arrow pointing down next to the pill ... a small dropdown menu. replacing the current one that hides all the guis"). Stop at rarity and Stop at quest ores; the HUD stays. Supersedes 2026-10-28's Auto-Roll settings window. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — UI notes]]
- **2026-10-31: Joiners arrive on the street, then go home** (village review; Claude's default). A hidden street spawn in front of N2 until the save loads and the plot is claimed, so nobody appears out by the world origin or in another player's yard. Respawns stay at home. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Village fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: Workspace holds only the game** (village review). The seven layout drafts, the x-2000 copy, GreatSwordRig and Sword Animations moved to `ServerStorage.Legacy.WorkspaceDrafts_2026_10_31` (Allen drags the rig back to edit animations). Bamboo, the stray Swordsmith, Weapon Rack and the rest wait for Allen. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Village fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The village is closed and lighter** (village review; Claude's defaults). Gate doors, walled lane exits and invisible guards over the town wall; lighter haze; each weather has its own light (rain dims, snow is cool), and colours are less saturated; empty lots keep their leaderboards; the mountains stay on phones; lamps 3 studs further out; no Neon chochin; the nameplate 5 studs higher. Warming the outdoor ambient is open. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Village fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: Other plots cost less to draw** (performance review; Claude's defaults). A neighbour's ore show plays only within 120 studs; furnace glow animates on each client; holder markers only on your plot; prop swords draw only once their station is bought, one etch at a time; the hotbar draws 2 viewports a slot (was 9). [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Performance fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: The style and accessibility reviews' fixes** (Claude's defaults). An amber selection edge for gamepads, focus back to the HUD on close; Q and Backspace close windows; every window X a bigger hit box with the same look; green buttons' words white; Mail's "Active only" a knob pill; HUD labels at least 14 px on a phone; the Gem Shop's four cards fit a 390 px phone. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — UI review fixes]]
- **2026-10-31: Reduce motion also stops coin fly-ins, the spotlight's glide and the station glow's pulse; a gamepad selects the spotlit button** (the settings and accessibility reviews; Claude's defaults). With motion off, coins add straight to the count and the value pill, the spotlight jumps, the glow holds steady, and the Auto-Roll and Auto Anvil icons stay still. The tutorial's spotlit button is selected once on a pad, so A presses it. Still moving: the strike ring's pulse, the buy bar's glow, the ghost's press, confetti, the build fly-in and the ore show's bob. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Reduced motion]]
- **2026-10-31: The delivery cart and rickshaws move as one welded piece** (performance review #6; Claude's default). One pivot a frame with the wheels turning through their welds, instead of every part; the cart is welded only while it's out, so it still builds itself in when bought. [[Design/Reviews 2026-10-31/Handoff — Cart and rickshaw welds]]
- **2026-11-01: Lucky Ore is an odds sign by the ore stands; the blueprint pads lose their saving bar; 50% More Coins leaves the shop floor** (Allen: "remove the lucky ore purchase next to the orres. instead replace it with a sign that shows the player the current chances of each ore, and then on the sign we can have it show the lucky ore purchase as well if they want it"; "remove the progress bar under the sword upgrades, its unnecessary"; "i dont want the physical 50% more coins purchase in the sword shop", "i still want it as a pass in the shop though"). The sign shows your chance of each rarity (rarities, not the 32 metals: Claude's default) with merges, Lucky Ore and rain counted, and while you could buy the pass a Lucky Ore column with its price and each chance with it; its Buy prompts the pass directly. Supersedes the glass case and the coins-vs-price bar of 2026-10-31. [[Sessions/2026-11-01]]
- **2026-11-01: Stone street; the street lamps lose their lights; Furnace 2 has no gate** (Allen: "i want stone street and can remove the extra lights. q9. no gate"). The road is cut stone (TownSlab), the lanes and open ground stay earth; the 12 Andon PointLights are gone, their Neon heads stay; Furnace 2 is buyable from the start under the pad cap. Supersedes 2026-10-22 "Earth street with a stone centre path, stone kept to compare" and 2026-10-31's Furnace 2 after Heating I. Allen also dropped the cut-off notes from 2026-10-30. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Stone street, no street lights, Furnace 2 ungated]]
- **2026-11-01: Crew level gems scale with progress** (Allen: "The gem price should also scale with how close they are to the next level"). Price = ceil(base × work left), at least 1; the server prices it and never charges more than the client showed. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Untitled 3 fixes: crew, Auto-Roll, signs, bays, NPC paths]]
- **2026-11-01: Rickshaw Bays are "Bay 1-3" with an effect line** (Allen: "people dont know what rickshaws are, just have it say Bay 1 ... need to show the benefits of it"). "+1 customer at once" under the name; an exception to one-line pad labels.
- **2026-11-01: Only the Auto-Roll knob toggles; the chevron is a tab under the pill** (Allen: "just make it activate on the pill itself").
- **2026-11-01: Crafter cards are coloured by stars** (Allen): bronze, blue, purple.
- **2026-11-01: Named characters walk in** (Allen picked real names over original look-alikes after being told the IP risk, and rare walk-ins). Clients ask for the sword that fits them and come only while it's on display (10% of regular walk-ins); half of VIPs are named. Their own lines. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Named customers and Swordsmith crafters]]
- **2026-11-01: Sword Crafters wear the Swordsmith model** (Allen: "ideally using the mask as well"): its clothes, hair, mask and gasa over each crafter's skin.
- **2026-11-01: Nothing a named character holds is kept** (Allen: "if they're holding an accessories remove them").
- **2026-11-01: Mail coins scale with shop upgrades** (Allen: "Yes, scale them"; "gems should still stay the same"): +10% per shop upgrade bought (racks, tables, Upgraded Store, Big Sword Stand, bays, Promoter, Cashier), 2.9× with all 19. Builds on the 2026-10-29 "pay like a sale" rule. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Mail scales with the shop, crafter looks]]
- **2026-11-01: Sword Crafters wear no hat, and look different** (Allen): Taise without the mask; Mei, Kenji and Rin their own hair; Rin (the top crafter) the Muscles Buff Body. Supersedes the gasa in "Sword Crafters wear the Swordsmith model".
- **2026-11-01: World pass labels show only at the prompt, name over price** (Allen: "too intrusive and large … should only show when they're close enough to buy").
- **2026-11-01: Rin is the bare-chested masked smith** (Allen's picture): no shirt or hair, grey hakama, white belt, barefoot. Supersedes Rin's own hair in "Sword Crafters wear no hat, and look different".
- **2026-11-01: The muscle crafter is Taru, in the Swordsmith's pants** (Allen: "Rename Rin to Taru"; "he has no pants and theres a white block there"). No belt. The save id stays `rin`. Supersedes "Rin is the bare-chested masked smith"'s grey hakama and white belt. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Taru, the lamp, Air, the bag, two more Big Sword Stands, the Ore Slot pad]]
- **2026-11-01: The Auto Ore Roller's lamp is lit only while Auto is on** (Allen).
- **2026-11-01: Air ingots are 30% see-through** (Allen: "a bit transparent"). Blades stay 45%.
- **2026-11-01: Bag: All / Swords / Ores tabs, 7 columns, smaller trash** (Allen). Tabs filter only the bag.
- **2026-11-01: Two more Big Sword Stands on the Upgraded Store's side walls** (Allen), 90,000 and 140,000 (Claude's), each after the one before; they count for mail pay.
- **2026-11-01: Ore Slot pads beside the lever** (Allen: "move the ore upgrade to where im standing").
- **2026-11-01: Taru wears a 3D black hakama** (Allen: "his ass cheeks are hanging out"): the catalog's Black Samurai Hakama Pants as layered clothing, white waist tie, bare legs under it. Supersedes "in the Swordsmith's pants". [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Taru's hakama]]
- **2026-11-01: Beginner's luck after the tutorial** (Allen: "just make the first 1 more likely ... we dont need to display it"): luck +3 on pulls after the tutorial until the first Rare or better is rolled; not shown on the odds sign. Claude's defaults: +3 and the end condition. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Beginner's luck]]
- **2026-11-01: Beginner's luck is +9** (Allen: "make it more likely to roll an epic"): Epic 40%, Legendary 10% at no merges (was +3, Epic 16%). Claude's number. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Beginner's luck]]
- **2026-11-01: The Auto-Roll arrow has no tab behind it** (Allen: "remove the background from the open button"). [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Auto-Roll arrow: no background, one spot]]
- **2026-11-01: Low poly mountains ring the village** (Allen: "i wanted it to be mountains around", "use blender for the mountains", "i want a low poly mountain"): three Blender ranges, 16 Persistent clones in two rows; replaces the stacked-box backdrop. Claude's defaults: sizes, colours, distances. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Low poly mountains around the map]]
- **2026-11-01: Part mountains in a rectangle** (Allen: "just use normal roblox parts ... more like rectangle surrounding the map"): 107 CornerWedge pyramids in three rows (green hills, rock, snow-capped rock) along a rectangle; replaces the Blender ranges. Claude's defaults: heights, colours, distances. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Part mountains in a rectangle]]
- **2026-11-01: Block mountains** (Allen: "make the mountains rectangular not actually like mountain shaped"): the rectangle of mountains is stacked rectangular blocks, not pyramids. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Part mountains in a rectangle]]
- **2026-11-01: Street lights are Allen's model** ("Use the streetlights model i added into Workspace for streetlights"): 10 on the main street walkways, one per segment each side, lantern toward the road. Claude's defaults: spacing and side offset. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Street lights from Allen's model]]
- **2026-11-01: Rickshaw bays have no sign** ("remove the sign on the rickshaw bays, and improve how they look"): cobbled slab, dark kerb, two bollards. Claude's defaults: the look. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Rickshaw bays: no sign, kerbed cobbles]]
- **2026-11-01: A gate at each end, no shrine** ("add the same gate we have on end of our map to replace the side with the red shrine"): the east end is a copy of the west gate, shut. The shrine, torii and lanterns are in Legacy. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#East gate replaces the shrine]]
- **2026-11-01: Day and night, short nights** (Allen: "night is much quicker than day. so night is only say 5 minutes"): 20-minute cycle, 15 min day, 5 min night, the same for everyone in a server; street lamps light at night. Claude's defaults: the 15-minute day, the night colours, lamps on past 30% night. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Day and night]]
- **2026-11-01: Yoshiwara preview built, not adopted** (Allen: "build it parallel to our current one so I can just compare"): a restyled copy at z +2000 with machiya rows, a maple median, lantern strings, an iron arch gate, a tower and dense rooftops. Claude's defaults: every look choice. Waiting on Allen. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Yoshiwara preview village]]
- **2026-11-01: Yoshiwara look dropped; spaced-lots preview built** (Allen: "Not a fan of it ... make a parallel map, and one where there's non-playable buildings between two players plots"): outer lots 28 studs out, three village houses in each 40-stud gap. Waiting on Allen. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Spaced-lots preview]]
- **2026-11-01: Keep the current village layout** (Allen: "nevermind just go back and use our current one"): no Yoshiwara look, no spaced lots; both previews deleted. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Spaced-lots preview]]
- **2026-11-01: Etching is held, not automatic** (Allen: "make etching non-automated so players have a role in that too and want to automate it as well"; picked "Hold to etch"): the drawing runs only while you hold, and the etch lands when it finishes. Supersedes 2026-10-20's "apply instantly". Resolves the parked manual-etching idea in [[Random thoughts]]. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Hold to etch, three coin etches]]
- **2026-11-01: Three etches, bought with coins** (Allen: "etches should be purchasable ... for now we can go with 3 solid ones"; picked coins): Flame +15%, Woven Wind +20%, Fracture +25% for 800 / 8,000 / 40,000 on pads by the Etching Table; Eclipse, Tide, Stormroot and Falling Stars retired. Claude's defaults: the picks, prices, bonuses and pads. Supersedes 2026-10-20's "both free for now" and "every pattern adds the same".
- **2026-11-01: Auto Etch and an Automation Pack** (Allen: "a pack that combines all the automation gamepasses"; picked adding an Auto Etcher): Auto Etch 49 R$; the pack (Auto Ore Roller, Auto Anvil, Auto Etch) 179 R$, shown while you own none of them, tagged Best Value. Claude's defaults: both prices. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Starter Pack as a product, Auto Etch, Automation Pack]]
- **2026-11-01: The Starter Pack is a one-time developer product** (Allen: "Starterpack should be a dev product, not game pass. but obviously single purchase only"): 99 R$, the old pass off sale. Supersedes the Monetization Plan §2's "Kind: a game pass".
- **2026-11-01: Best Value tags are honest** (Allen: "look into a decoy purchase in shop and a best value label"): the tag goes on the pack with the most per Robux, from live prices. A decoy is a proposal only: [[Design/Shop Pricing and Showcase Proposals — 2026-11-01]].
- **2026-11-01: Each crafter dressed differently; the courier is Riku** (Allen: "make swordsmiths wear different clothes per person", "delivery person needs to be dressed up"). Claude's defaults: every outfit, Haru keeping the Swordsmith's. [[Sessions/2026-11-01#Crafter outfits, the courier dressed up]]
- **2026-11-02: The 200 Gems pack is a decoy** (Allen: "Fuck your recommendation of going against decoy pricing. I want it."): 399 R$ (was 229), so 500 Gems at 449 is the deal; option B of [[Design/Shop Pricing and Showcase Proposals — 2026-11-01]]. The not-enough-gems popup never offers it (`Store.DECOYS`). Supersedes 2026-11-01's "a decoy is a proposal only". [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Decoy pricing]]
- **2026-11-02: Showcase Stands are built** (Allen: "Yes build thhe showcase stands"): three upright stands on the street left of the shop, 3,000 / 25,000 / 90,000 after Display Table 1, display only; each filled one brings walk-ins 5% sooner, 10% with an Epic+ metal or an etched and polished sword. Claude's defaults: the spot (the forecourt by the door is ~3 studs deep under the roof, too tight), prices and pull. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Showcase Stands]]
- **2026-11-02: Auto Etch has a switch** (Allen: "is auto-etching enabled by default if gamepass is on? It should be togglable as well like the auto forge at the anvil"): the Auto Anvil's pill in the etching screen (L1 on a pad), saved as `autoEtch`, off until switched on; bought at the pill it switches on. Claude's default: off at first, as the anvil. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Auto Etch switch]]
- **2026-11-02: A Dev Panel switch for passes** (Allen: "i need a way to remove all my gamepasses so i can actually playtest for real"): Studio-only "Passes: on/off" drops every pass for the session and gives them back; StoreService's `StudioLocked` does it for a whole Play. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Playing without passes]]
- **2026-11-02: The Sword Crafter costs 20,000** (Allen: "do 20,000 for sword crafter"; was 40,000). Between today's 40,000 and the proposed 15,000 of [[Design/Earlier First Crafter — 2026-11-02]]. Sim [[Development/Sim Runs/crafter-20k-2026-11-02]] (100 players a type, 8 h): Taise at 1:04 / 1:15 / 1:25 for manager / efficient / grinder players, 2:59 casual (target 1 h 30). Supersedes 2026-10-30's 40,000. The Stocker stays 36,000.
- **2026-11-02: The Stocker costs 12,000** (Allen: "Yes, both" to [[Design/Earlier First Crafter — 2026-11-02]]; was 36,000), still after the Sword Crafter, so his swords don't wait hours on a full rack. The Sword Crafter stays 20,000 (built earlier the same day). Sim of 15k + 12k: casual crew swords +40%, the full-rack wait 6.1 → 1.9 h ([[Development/Sim Runs/crafter-price-2026-11-02]]). Supersedes "The Stocker stays 36,000".
- **2026-11-02: Upgrade gates overhauled** (Allen: "we need to overhaul the whole upgrades progression system. it seems too tangled and things are locked behind other upgrades that just don't make sense", then "work on those"). Rule: an upgrade waits only for the one before it in its line, or for what it's used with; swords gate only swords; price paces; staff past the first hire wait for the Sword Crafter. Gone: Ingot Rack after Ore Slot 2, Faster Strikes I after the Wakizashi, Bay 1 after Display Table 1 and the Katana, Polishing Station 2 after the Etching Table, Shop Promoter after Display Table 1 and the Longsword (now the Sword Crafter), Cashier after the Stocker (now the Sword Crafter), Etching Table 2 after the Sword Rack and Gladius, Kenji's and Taru's blueprint needs, every Deliveries level gate. Kept: Anvil 2 after Furnace 2, Furnace 3 after Anvil 3, Haru after Furnace 3 (Allen: "the furnace is for the crafter"). **The Upgraded Store costs 15,000** (was 6,000; Allen 2026-11-01: "seems a bit quick to get to"). Supersedes the stage gates of 2026-10-28 still left. [[Design/Upgrade Tree Review — 2026-11-01#Built 2026-11-02]]
- **2026-11-02: Target the first Sword Crafter at about 15 min of casual play** (Allen, after recording Create Pottery to its first employee: "it took around 15 minutes of casual play. I think that's a target I also want for my first sword crafter"). Replaces the Crafter 1:30 of 2026-10-29. In Pottery the Apprentice ($900) is 55% of 15-min income behind one $200 shape; ours is 6.3× a casual player's with ~6,940 of stations first. Prices, gates and the sim's Crafter target (`report.TARGETS`, `time_model.ANCHORS`) are unchanged until the new path is picked. [[Design/Pottery Playthrough 2026-11-02]]
- **2026-11-02: The Sword Crafter costs 2,000 and brings Anvil 2** (Allen: "Yes, build it" to hire first at 2,000, from [[Development/Sim Runs/crafter-15min-2026-11-02]]). He waits only for the Ingot Rack and Sword Rack; Anvil 2 has no pad of its own; Furnace 2 is an ordinary pad. Supersedes the Sword Crafter 20,000 after Anvil 2 (3,400, after the Ingot Rack, Furnace 2 and Sword Rack) of the same day. The sim's Crafter target is 15 min. What draws casual players to him (a tutorial step, a pinned goal, the pad) is still open. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#The 15-minute Sword Crafter built]]
- **2026-11-02: The 15-minute Sword Crafter is a reach, not a must** (Allen: "players dont necessarily have to buy the swordcrafter", to [[Design/Crafter Pull Options — 2026-11-02]]). The target means his pad stands and he's affordable by about 15 min (casual: pad at 12 min, 2,000 earned since by 17). It doesn't mean everyone hires him then. No letter, guide step or pad hold pulls players to him.
- **2026-11-02: A label over your head for what you hold** (Allen: "I want to be able to view my sword similar to how these pots/clay are viewed when held"; asked, "Label, keep the grip"). It shows the blade, the metal in its rarity colour and the grade; for an ingot, its name and rarity. Only you see yours. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Gameplay enhancements]]
- **2026-11-02: Display Tables 2–4 before the Upgraded Store, and the store after Table 2** (Allen: "Upgraded Store comes too quickly. while other racks and tables aren't available ... the other tables/racks in the store should still be able to be bought"; asked, "Also make the store later"). Tables 2–4 stand on the starter floor; Table 5 waits for the store. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Gameplay enhancements]]
- **2026-11-02: Every coin price ends in 0** (Allen: "round up or down to the nearest 10"): Ore Slot 2 80, Wall Sword Rack 2 110, merged Ore Slots rounded. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Gameplay enhancements]]
- **2026-11-02: A banner when the weather changes** (Allen: "Weather changes should have a brief popup message that shows what the weather is and what the boost is"): "[Sunny] 2x Customers" at the top for 4 s, like Create Pottery's "[Heatwave] 2x Kiln Surprises". [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Gameplay enhancements]]
- **2026-11-02: The crafter uses your furnace only while it's idle** (Allen: "Only when it's idle"): a crafter skips a furnace you've put in or taken out of in the last 30 s, including your forgotten ingot. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#The crafter uses your furnace only while it's idle]]
- **2026-11-02: Old Studio backups live outside the place** (Allen: "Fix all besides 2", on the code audit): the 352 backups in `ServerStorage.Legacy` and the unused free UI pack went to `.rbxm` files in `Archive/Studio Backups/` (kept off GitHub). `Legacy` keeps only the current round of `_before…` copies and `SharedV1`. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Audit and its fixes]]
- **2026-11-02: Cancelling a delivery order counts as a skip** (same audit): its slot waits 5 min like a skipped offer, so accepting and cancelling no longer re-rolls the offers for free. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Audit and its fixes]]
- **2026-11-02: A big sword the crew can't hang goes back to your hands** (same audit): as when you rack it yourself, instead of sitting on the rack unseen. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Audit and its fixes]]
- **2026-11-02: The odds panels stay as they are for now** (Allen left audit finding 2 out of "Fix all besides 2"): the Shop window's odds leave out Rain (the Lucky Ore sign includes it), and neither shows the hidden +9 beginner luck. Revisit before the Lucky Ore pass goes on sale. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Audit and its fixes]]
- **2026-11-02: Hotbar slots are style H6** (Allen picked it from two sheets of styles): a soft rarity-colour gradient, the collection's dot halftone fading in from a corner, a thin black outline and a white inner rim, no hand-drawn `EdgeArt`. Supersedes 2026-10-30's dark rarity sheen. [[Sessions/2026-11-02#Hotbar style H6]]
- **2026-11-03: Swords on display wear a shop shine** (Allen picked it from live mock-ups, "make the swords pop more while theyre on the store racks"): a thin silver line on the cutting edge that follows your viewing angle and holds still while you do; mirror-polished displayed swords also pulse along it every few seconds, in place of the glare blob. Held swords keep the glare. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Shop shine on displayed swords]]
- **2026-11-03: The ore lever clunks, then ratchets** (Allen picked a pitched-down clockwork wind-up): a metal clunk at the pull and the wind-up as the arm springs back, a little higher or lower each pull. No slot-machine bells. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Lever sounds]]
- **2026-11-03: Buttons click and hover** (Allen's sound picks): every UI button clicks when pressed and ticks on mouse hover (not on touch); new buy and coin sounds, the buy one quieter. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#UI, buy and coin sounds]]
- **2026-11-03: A roll ends on the ingots' clinks, no ring; one music track; new swing whoosh** (Allen's picks). [[Sessions/2026-11-03#UI, buy and coin sounds]]
- **2026-11-03: Polishing is scrubbing** (Allen picked option 1): a round brush wipes a grime skin off the blade anywhere, in any motion; each station's saved polish is how clean its strip is, so the save, server check and value stay one number per station. The rest snaps clean at 95%. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Polishing by scrubbing]]
- **2026-11-03 (later): A roll ends quietly** (Allen: "just remove the clink after the ingot drops too"): no end chime and no landing clink; supersedes "ends on the ingots' clinks" above. **Music at half volume** (0.25; "music needs to be turned down"). [[Sessions/2026-11-03#UI, buy and coin sounds]]

- **2026-11-03: Auto Anvil seats the hilt too** (Allen: "auto-anvil should also include the hilting"; picked the hands-off option). Supersedes "the hilt seat stays by hand". [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: Flame comes with the Etching Table; every etch drops 5** (Allen: "buying etching table should automatically come with the 1st etch", "flame should give base +10%"; picked "shift all down 5"): Flame +10% free, Woven Wind +15% (8,000), Fracture +20% (40,000). Supersedes 2026-11-01's 15 / 20 / 25 and Flame's 800 pad. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: Daily reward tiles by tier** (Allen: "daily rewards need to feel more special"; picked Claude's default): brown, blue, purple, then a big gold day 7; gem icons grow to a pile and a chest; claimed days checked and dimmed. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: Held swords glint with a slow sweep** (Allen: "held swords should have different glint effect"; picked Claude's default); the rack keeps the shop shine. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: Customers in the top bar** (Allen: "current customers in the store would be a good stat to have ... could use the topbar, and have a customer icon with a small number below it. when pressed it just describes"): a TopbarPlus icon, the Full Body figure, whose number is the plot's customers in or on their way; a tap spells it out for 2.5 s. Claude's defaults: the icon, the number beside it (TopbarPlus's label), the wording. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: A 1.5 s pause after a decline** (Allen: "after declining an offer, there should be a pause before they offer more or leave"; length Claude's). [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: The Shop window wears the seigaiha background** (Allen: "make the background of shop the same japanese background of other windows"); supersedes the dark Shop fill. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: Tutorial ending and the crew tour** (Allen: "after buying shop boost with gems, tutorial ends. the upgrades should close automatically", "add confetti after tutorial is finished", "after buying first sword crafter, should spotlight the crew menu"): the Gem Shop closes on the last buy, confetti bursts (not with Reduce motion), and the first hire spotlights the Crew button, Taise's card and Swords. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: Woven Wind's pad always stands once the Etching Table is bought** (from the code review, Claude's default): as Flame's pad did before Flame came with the table, so the etch chain doesn't wait under the pad cap. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: The buy-upgrade pad keeps its sound** (Allen: "Keep it", on the note's "remove/change the buy upgrade pad sound"): `Cues.Upgrade` stays 80736831159506 at 0.3. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Fix list from the sound-ids note]]
- **2026-11-03: A training dummy in the smithy yard, just for fun** (Allen: "just a quirky decoration, players can hit it and it does a little animation"; like +1 simulators', "you stand close to it and ... it gets automatically hit"; picked "No, just for fun" and "Smithy yard"): standing still beside it you slash it on a beat and it wobbles; no stat, no reward, no save. Claude's defaults: the look, the corner by the workshop door, reach 5.5, a slash every 0.65 s, the wobble on your own screen only. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Training dummy]]
- **2026-11-03: The sword racks' stretcher sits against the posts** (Allen: "the stretcher on the swordrack is not attached to the the post"): moved back to overlap them, clear of the blades. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Sword rack stretcher on its posts]]
- **2026-11-03: Buying an upgrade steps you out only of a solid part you're in** (Allen: "sometimes buying upgrade in shop teleports me outside the shop"): not out of the item's whole box, which for the Upgraded Store was the shop. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Notes batch: teleport, gem prices, Ingot Racks 3-4]]
- **2026-11-03: Gem Shop prices climb gently** (Allen: "the gem upgrades you buy go up way too much per buy"; picked the gentle curve): 990 gems for all twenty levels (was ~3,680); the first Better Advertising stays 5. Supersedes 2026-10-28's prices and "Better Advertising II + More Clients I cost the Starter Pack's 100 gems". [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Notes batch: teleport, gem prices, Ingot Racks 3-4]]
- **2026-11-03: Ingot Racks 3 and 4** (Allen: "add more ingot rack upgrades"; picked two more shelves, +6 each): 8,000 and 30,000 in the Ingot Rack chain, along the same wall; 24 ingots at the max. Prices Claude's. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Notes batch: teleport, gem prices, Ingot Racks 3-4]]
- **2026-11-03: The crew Station page is "<Name>'s Station", tap an anvil to toggle** (Allen: "no equip or unequip just clicking the anvil should be the toggle. Add the swordkeepers face into the anvil"): Back at the top, each anvil card showing its worker's portrait. **Crew cards are portrait cards, 2 across** (Allen picked it; "this feels like a waste of space"). Claude's call, pending Allen: the hire comparison bars left the roster cards. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Crew window: Station page and portrait cards (subagent)]]
- **2026-11-03: The crew cards stay as they were** (Allen: "i dont like it revert, the crew shop layout changes"; kept the Station page): supersedes the portrait cards, 2 across, above; the hire comparison bars are back. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Crew window: Station page and portrait cards (subagent)]]
- **2026-11-03: Ingot Racks 3-4 face Racks 1-2 across the aisle; all ingot racks are open shelves** (Allen: "have them across from the two racks. also can we remove the back from the shelf and also the Top"): no back or top, the sides end at the top shelf. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Ingot racks: across the aisle, open shelves]]
- **2026-11-03: Crafters make two swords a trip and walk faster with level; their Stats page shows swords a minute** (Allen: "batch and level speed and show this stat in the crew window stats as well"): three Lv. 5 crafters made ~2.5 swords a minute against ~4-4.7 buyers with max ads and the Promoter, two thirds of it walking. `BATCH` 2; walk 6 → 12 (Taise, Haru), 7 → 13 (Mei, Kenji), 8 → 14 (Taru); Swords / Min and Crew / Min rows on Stats. Supersedes "the crafters keep their roster walk speeds" (they now grow from them). [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Crafters: two swords a trip, faster with level, swords a minute in Stats]]
- ~~**2026-11-03: plots stand a step up**~~ superseded 2026-11-04 (only the shop building is raised); the dry gutters stand. **2026-11-03: No water in the street's gutters; plots stand a step up** (Allen: "can we remove the water on the roads. and make the players plot/shop raised so you need to take a step up to get on it"): dry stone gutters; each plot on a 1-stud stone plinth from the kerb back, its forecourt raised with it; the rickshaw bays stay on the road; the delivery cart has a ramp over the gutter. Claude's calls: 1 stud, the plinth in the kerbs' stone, a ramp only where the cart crosses (people step up). [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Map style: dry gutters, plots a step up]]
- **2026-11-04: Only the shop building stands a step up, not the plot** (Allen: "i meant only the building, not the plot of land"): the land, rickshaw bays, cart route and street lamps are back at street level; the shop (Starter and Upgraded) with everything inside, its back wall and its buy pads is 1 stud up on a stone plinth, a step up at the front door and a step down at the back door to the yard. Claude's calls: 1 stud, the plinth in the kerb stone, the awning area and street showcases left on the street. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#The shop building a step up (not the plot)]]
- **2026-11-04: Walkways and curbs take Allen's new look across the map** (Allen: "i also set the walkways and curb a diff texture, match it across the map"): walkways Slate `PaintFloor2`, curbs Pebble `PaintEarth`, colours unchanged. Claude's call: the bays' kerbs, the gutters and the shop plinths keep the old stone. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Walkways and curbs in Allen's new look]]
- **2026-11-04: Rickshaw bays get a stone pad, a curb-stone frame and two wooden corner posts with a little lantern** (Allen: "Can we make the rickshaws bays look better."): Claude's design, in the walkway, curb, shop wood and roof-tile looks; nothing new on the rickshaw's path. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Rickshaw bays restyled]]
- **2026-11-04: Crew carry in hand, and carry more with level** (Allen: "i want the crew to hold the sword or ingot they're holding while walking between stations. we can remove the hammer that they're always holding"; "have the stocker be able to hold 2 swords at a time"; "have the sword capacity grow as their level increases, both for sword crafters and stockers"): crafters and the Stocker carry 2 a trip at Lv. 1-2, 3 at Lv. 3-4, 4 at Lv. 5; the ingots and swords show in the right hand; the hammer only while striking. Claude's calls: the 2/3/4 steps; crafters use either Polishing Station and heat their ingots in free furnaces at once (both were holding them back). Open: making Taru and Kenji faster per sword than Mei. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Crew carry what they hold; bigger loads with level]]
- **2026-11-04: Mei, Kenji and Taru are faster on every step** (Allen chose "Faster on every step"): each crafter has its own forge, polish and walk times, Taru forging in 6 s, polishing in 2.5 and walking 16 at Lv. 5 against Mei's 11, 4 and 13; Taise and Haru unchanged. Claude's numbers, aiming at Taru about 1.4-1.5x Mei. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Top crafters faster on every step]]
- **2026-11-04: Four ores change tier** (Allen picked them from Claude's proposal): Marble to Common, Rose Gold and Obsidian to Rare, Ruby to Epic; Amethyst stays Rare. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Ore tiers re-sorted]]
- **2026-11-04: Each Showcase Stand sword has its own pull, shown over the stand when you're close** (Allen: "each sword can have a certain pull amount that affects your stores stats", "the sword type can be part of it", "show near stand when close"): metal tier 2-10%, +1% a sword-type step, +1% polished, +1% etched; the picker shows each sword's pull. Supersedes 2026-11-02's 5% / 10%. Claude's numbers. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Showcase Stands: each sword's pull, shown]]
- **2026-11-04: The plot's mailbox goes** (Allen: "remove this mailbox"): it was only decoration; mail stays on the HUD's Mail button. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Stand labels, mailbox, rickshaw, delivery bay]]
- **2026-11-04: The delivery bay gets a gate roof, lanterns, a sign and a cart shelter** (Allen: "make the delivery bay a bit more noticable"): Claude's design in the shop's materials; the yard paved like the walkways. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Stand labels, mailbox, rickshaw, delivery bay]]
- **2026-11-04: Mihawk, Zoro and Giyu wear their character's catalog clothes and hair** (Allen: "this NPC has no clothes or anything it's too bland"): Claude's picks from the catalog; Trunks waits for a hair item. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#VIP customers dressed]]
- **2026-11-04: The street gets festival dressing** (Allen: "feels like it's missing some extra decorations"): lantern strings across the road, nobori banners, barrel groups, tea benches and plaza food stalls; built props only, Claude's picks and placement. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#Street dressing: lanterns, banners, barrels, benches, stalls]]
- **2026-11-04: New ores at Claude's tiers, Frost Iron renamed Ice** (Allen: "just throwing some names out Diamond, Pearl, Void, Nimbus, Ice, Candy, Emerald, Sakura, Tempest, Panda, Gunmetal", then "Use these tiers", "Rename Frost Iron to Ice", "Tint ones first"): Gunmetal, Pearl Uncommon; Emerald, Sakura, Nimbus Epic; Diamond, Void, Tempest Legendary; Candy, Panda Secret. Gunmetal, Emerald and Diamond built first; the textured seven after. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#New ores: Ice, Gunmetal, Emerald, Diamond]]
- **2026-11-04: Diamond gets its own faceted texture** (Allen: "Diamond doesnt look diamond enough", then picked "Faceted texture" over cyan glass): Roblox Glass ignores MaterialVariants, so it's a Metal-based cut-facet look. [[Sessions/2026-11-04#New ores: Ice, Gunmetal, Emerald, Diamond]]
- **2026-11-04: Diamond is the icy-blue faceted one** (Allen: "3 icy blue", from white / slightly see-through / icy blue): Metal look with the `Diamond` texture, tint (215, 240, 255). [[Sessions/2026-11-04#New ores: Ice, Gunmetal, Emerald, Diamond]]

- **2026-11-07: Three post-launch updates proposed, none decided** (Allen: "Come up with 3 major updates we can use for the game. Keep in mind the type of game we have."): Legendary Commissions (secret-letter tiers and VIP commission chains for 4–20 h), Village Festival (seasonal festival metals, orders, cosmetics, a Smith's Season) and Trade Routes (delivery clients, rush orders, delivery rewards, capped away earnings), in the proposed order Trade Routes → Commissions → Festival, with eight questions for Allen. Claude's picks from the vault's recorded ideas; nothing built. [[Design/Major Updates Plan — 2026-11-07]]
- **2026-11-07: Windows must end above the bottom of a 388-px phone with their 29 px top-bar offset** (from Allen's UI audit ask: "look for padding issues where there's overlapping UI or UI that had no padding between them"): the Store, Deliveries and Collection windows were 10–21 px past it and are 329 px tall now (Claude's defaults: Store padding 15 / 10 and gap 11, Deliveries body 372 and fit width 930, Collection fit height 520). Open for Allen: on 360-px-tall phones every big window still runs 14–24 px past the bottom at the 0.7 floor (a lower floor, scrolling bodies or shorter layouts). [[Design/Reviews 2026-11-07/UI Audit]]
- **2026-11-07: Decorative parts cast no shadow under 1.5 studs, take no touch, and never-colliding meshes use Box collision** (Claude's defaults from the performance audit, every change undoable from a `PerfAudit1107_*` attribute): the street's lantern brackets, the plot template's 622 small parts, 968 sword and prop meshes; SurfaceGuis cull at 80–350 studs; the lever hand's Humanoid runs no state machine. The audit's other proposals (a slimmer SmithState, fewer shine beams, held NPCs, a blur cap, PersistentPerPlayer stations) wait for Allen. [[Design/Reviews 2026-11-07/Performance Audit]]
- **2026-11-07: The whole map goes through the coplanar check after a day of moves, and only 0.04–0.10 trims fix what it finds** (the standing no-z-fighting rule applied to today's plots-30-apart, narrower-street, taller-walls and shop-left moves; Claude's defaults for which face moves): 24 parts trimmed (the sign-frame posts under their beams, the Upgraded Store's side posts and back upper posts, four wall caps), each keeping its old CFrame and Size in `MapPass1107_*` attributes; the Toolbox lamps' 3,277 internal slivers and the cart bed's 0.03 slivers accepted as before. Left for Allen: the kept forest stones and one tree floating over the grass, the seven map proposals. [[Design/Reviews 2026-11-07/Map Pass]]
- **2026-11-07: Client loops do nothing while idle, and phones on Low graphics get no menu blur** (Claude's defaults from the performance audit's P8 and P7; reversible from the `_beforeIdleGate_1107` / `_beforeBlurCap_1107` backups): PolishController's two loops return early with no station open, TycoonController's pad check runs at 10 Hz, `HudFocus` skips the blur at `SavedQualityLevel` 1–3 (Automatic keeps it). [[Sessions/2026-11-07#Performance proposals P8 and P7 applied (idle loops gated, blur skipped on Low)]]
- **2026-11-08: A failed save snapshot skips only that player, and nothing after a pack's grant can stop its receipt being recorded** (Allen: "Yes, fix both", the code audit's P1 and receipt findings; Claude's way: `SmithyCore.safeSnapshot` in leave / autosave / BindToClose, pcall around StoreService's logging and push). Backups `_beforeSaveGuard_1108`. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Code audit P1 and receipt fix applied]]
- **2026-11-08: Beginner's luck is not shown in Lucky Ore's odds** (Allen: "the paid luck boosts apply to paid items, our is not that. it doesn't need to be disclosed the odds for the first pull."), confirming his 2026-11-01 call against the pre-release checklist's blocker. The panels' missing rain luck and the wanted-metal pull are still open. [[Design/Reviews 2026-11-07/Pre-release Checklist]]
- **2026-11-08: Managed pricing on for every pass and product** (Allen: "Managed pricing should be on for every purchase"). Waits for Allen to accept the managed pricing terms in Creator Hub once; Open Cloud then sets `isManagedPricingEnabled` on all 27.
- **2026-11-08: The pull toward a letter's metal is tutorial-only, and the Shop odds panel shows only permanent luck** (Allen: "i want it to only be in the tutorial, not every 2 rolls is providing the right material for the quset"; "why would shop panels odd include a temporary luck boost?"). `ForgeService.rollFor` skips `wantedMetal` once `TutorialDone`; no change for players today (only the Iron Tantō letter names a metal). [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Wanted-metal pull: the tutorial only; rain luck off the blocker list]]
- **2026-11-08: A building item steps you out when it lands, on your screen too** (Allen: "i buy an upgrade and run into the table while building and get stuck"). The server's step-out at the buy stays; the client repeats it when the build-in lands. Claude's call: a copy in TycoonController, not a shared module. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Stuck in a table that builds in]]
- **2026-11-08: The delivery bay gets a packing bench, a crate stack that grows with Capacity, and a hitch set for an ox** (Allen: "Top 3, i'll find a model for the ox"). Claude's picks: the bench's bundles show the swords packed into the queued order (the cart's crates show the active one), crates 3/5/7/9 at Capacity 0-3, the hitch set by the gate; Allen brings the ox model. Route map, side door and ground details not chosen. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Delivery bay dressing: packing bench, Capacity crates, hitch set]]
- **2026-11-08: The delivery bay runs back to the workshop** (Allen: "extend the length of the delivery bay so it starts closer to the workshop"). The stone yard reaches plot z 1.5, the board stands at the workshop's edge, the cart parks 12 further in (its route unchanged on the street). [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Delivery bay lengthened toward the workshop]]
- **2026-11-08: No ox or horse for the delivery cart** (Allen: "i think we can forget about the ox/horse"). Riku keeps pulling the cart; the hitch post, trough and hay bale stay as dressing. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Delivery bay dressing: packing bench, Capacity crates, hitch set]]
- **2026-11-08: Manual packing is prototyped, not decided** (Allen: "prototype the manual packing so i know what it looks like"): hold to roll cloth, tap two ties on gold marks, clean or sloppy; Dev panel only, no pay or items. Claude's tuning (1.3 s roll, ±7% marks). Whether it ships, and the Packer crew, wait for Allen's playtest. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Packing prototype]]
- **2026-11-08: The packing prototype hides your character and uses a part-built stand-in sword** (Allen: "my head is blocking it"). Claude's fix; the stand-in replaces the baked katana blade mesh, whose bounds put it sideways off the bench. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Packing prototype: first playtest fixes]]
- **2026-11-08: Packing is prototyped five ways; the cloth wrap is dropped** (Allen: "not a big fan of this motion", then "all of them"): Box (drag into slots, lid, nails), Puzzle (fit lengths in a 10 x 3 grid), Carry (walk crates to the cart), Stamp (hold the hanko), Straw (tap fast). Dev panel only; which one ships waits for Allen's playtest. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Packing prototype: five modes]]
- **2026-11-08: The packing prototype uses your real swords, in crates sized to them** (Allen: "can we not prototype with actual swords?"). Claude's rules: 0.5-stud cells, two rows for blades wider than ~0.56 studs, the longest sword sets a crate's length, at most 4 rows, the rest go in the next crate; Box outlines match each sword's size. Still a Dev panel prototype. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Packing prototype: real swords]]
- **2026-11-08: The packing prototype measures swords from their blade data, not their meshes** (Allen: "theres no outline?"). Claude's fix: baked blade meshes have padded square bounds and a put-away sword's Handle drifts; footprints now come from BladeCore's outline and the Handle frame from the drawn blade. Big swords stay out (as on the cart). [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Packing prototype: true sword sizes]]
- **2026-11-08: The etch colour is picked on a colour field, not from eight presets** (Allen: "use a color wheel or picker instead of having pre-set colors"; "Yes, build it"). Claude's design: hue across, saturation down, full brightness only so every etch reads, snapped to 97 colours to bound the etch image cache; Metal stays the default. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Etch colour field]]
- **2026-11-08: The etch colour knob follows the pointer; only its colour snaps** (Allen: "unclear that its supposed to be the color im holding"). Claude's fix, with the 58 px top-bar offset removed. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Etch colour field: knob under the cursor]]
- **2026-11-08: The etch colour is chosen during the show, from a button beside Hold, and can change until the drawing is done** (Allen: "a button next to the hold and it opens up a color wheel. you can open it up at the start in the middle, end whenever"). Supersedes the colour window after the pattern (same day). Claude's details: the swatch-and-rainbow-ring button, Metal beside the field, the camera sliding up while it's open, the next show starting on the last colour. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Etch colour button in the show]]
- **2026-11-08: Etch colours are a grid of 36 swatches, not a colour field** (Allen: "how about we just have a grid of the colors that players can choose from"). Claude's palette: 12 hues in vivid, soft and pastel rows, plus Metal; still bright only. The button beside Hold and the live recolour stay. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Etch colours as a swatch grid]]
- **2026-11-08: The etch colour no longer colours the hilt; the Flame guard keeps the hilt's own colours** (Allen: "start with 1", of keeping hilt colours fixed vs a separate hilt colour picker). Supersedes the 2026-10-20 rule that an etch's guard takes the etch colours; the guard's shape still comes from the pattern. A hilt colour picker stays an idea: hilt colours show tier and value. The reset option reads **Default** (Allen: "just have it say Default"). [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Etch colour leaves the hilt alone; Default]]

- **2026-11-08: Anvil sparks grow with each strike and are bigger** (Allen: the anvil "doesn't really feel too immersive", then "maybe just more sparks?"; no hit-stop, which he felt would slow forging). 10 sparks on the first blow up to 46 on the 10th, doubled on the last, plus the Faster Strikes bonus; bigger, faster and wider. Claude's numbers. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#More anvil sparks]]

- **2026-11-08: The anvil starts from the real ingot, at its size** (Allen: "the steps aren't drastic of a change enough? also we start with a rectangular block thats small and not like the ingot", then "Yes, real ingot"). The blank is the ingot's 58.65 × 30.6 × 19.1 cm (was 10 × 5.2 × 3.2, a sixth); the ingot model lies on the anvil until the first blow; the camera frames it, then closes in after the lengthen stage. Short blades get shorter as they're forged. Supersedes the 2026-10-28 blank's size. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#The anvil starts from the real ingot]]

- **2026-11-08: The anvil's ingot keeps its shape but fits the anvil, centred; every stage takes metal off** (Allen: "the ingot is too big, i was thinking it keeps its shape but still fits the anvil"; "the intermediate steps/hits the changes feel smaller"). Blank 0.65 of the ingot (1.91 × 0.99 × 0.62 studs), centred until the first blow; the lengthened and preform shapes keep 45% and 15% of the ingot's extra bulk (Claude's numbers). Supersedes this morning's full-size blank and camera ease. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#The ingot fits the anvil; every blow takes metal off]]
- **2026-11-08: The tutorial's end cheer has a sound** (Allen: "can we use this sound 73743814975286 after the tutorial and the confetti is shown"): "Confetti Celebration" plays with the banner and confetti, also with Reduce motion. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Tutorial-end sound]]
- **2026-11-08: Thumbnails start from seven written concepts; the forging close-up is staged first** (Allen: "help me refine them so I can pass them to an image generator", "some of them I think we can actually model out in Studio", then "4: forging close-up"). Claude's defaults: no text in generator prompts (added after), only what's in the game, no anime characters, a Studio capture as each generator's reference; the forging shot uses a finished polished katana on the anvil. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08]] [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Thumbnail concept 4 staged in Studio]]
- **2026-11-08: Taru's thumbnail is a double-biceps flex beside a planted Zanbatō; the "vs" is Taise against Taru** (Allen: "Yes, Taru next"). Claude's defaults: the Zanbatō in Magma Steel planted tip-down rather than held (reads as a giant sword without a grip); Taise, the first crafter, with a plain iron tantō as the weak side; both keep the mask. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08#3. Taru, the muscle crafter]]
- **2026-11-08: Thumbnail 5c: Rengoku walks into the shop and the bacon-hair cashier is shocked** (Allen: "give me the prompt for the cashier view where rengoku walks into the store and the cashier is shocked", then "i want the bacon hair boy to be the cashier in the thumbnail"). Supersedes the "no anime characters" thumbnail rule for this shot (Allen's call; Claude flagged the copyright and moderation risk). Claude's default: the counter copy brought up to the front door for the reference. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08#5. Behind the counter]]
- **2026-11-08: A thumbnail staging map in Workspace that Allen edits himself, deleted at runtime** (Allen: "can you create a staging map that i can edit myself and move them around"). Claude's defaults: in the real map's yard (so shots get the real background) rather than a separate place; one anchored model per character or prop with fixed poses; a server Script destroys `Workspace.ThumbnailStaging` when the game runs, so it never reaches Play or live. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08#Staging map (Studio)]]
- **2026-11-08: The thumbnail staging map gets a fully upgraded shop with filled displays** (Allen: "i meant also create the store so i can move my camera around. and fill the racks and tables with actual good looking swords"). Claude's defaults: a copy of the plot on the empty lot N1 (the real village around it), built as TycoonService/PlotService would with every upgrade bought; 39 mirror-polished swords, every blueprint/metal different, rare to legendary metals, Gold or Gem hilts, no etches; the cast moved into that copy's yard; the workshop Sword Rack left empty. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08#Staging map (Studio)]]
- **2026-11-08: The staging shop's workshop Sword Rack is full of the priciest swords** (Allen: "can you fill the sword rack with expensive swords too"). Claude's defaults: the Fictional blueprints in legendary metals on hooks 1–4 and 7–10, a Diamond flamberge and a Soulfire claymore across hooks 5–6 and 11–12. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08#Staging map (Studio)]]
- **2026-11-08: Thumbnail 3c: Taise and Taru standing side by side, no poses** (Allen: "can you set up a shot of taise and taru next to each other. no poses, just standing"). Claude's defaults: on the street in front of the staging shop, Taru left, both facing the camera; a close and a wide (sign in view) reference. [[Design/Thumbnail Concepts — 2026-11-08#3. Taru, the muscle crafter]]
- **2026-11-08: A held mirror-polished sword wears a still fill of its own colour, so it reads polished at every camera angle** (Allen: "any way we can have them always look reflective? sometimes they look dull even under the sun", then "Yes, 0.5 fill" from the test renders). Measured first: metalness 80 / 50 / 30 % and roughness 0.19–0.22 look the same off the sun angle (the blade shows the sky). Claude's defaults: held swords only (Highlights cap at 31 on screen; displayed swords keep the shop shine), plain metals only (textured blades would wash out). It does not move, so the no-glint-on-held-swords decisions stand. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Mirror steel at every angle (test)]]
- **2026-11-08: Bought items build in without taking the floor away or blocking you** (Allen: "upgrading the store causes me to fall down and clip with the floor", then "are the parts non-collidable? that would probably help"). What you could stand on (top within 1.5 studs of the item's foot) fades in where it is; flying pieces pass through characters until they land. Claude's defaults: a collision group rather than CanCollide (the server restores CanCollide at the unlock), 1.5 studs. [[Sessions/2026-11-08#Upgraded Store: no fall through the floor]]
- **2026-11-08: The Deliveries trip steps sit on the window with no panel when no order is on; Upgrade cart is a bevelled button with the green upgrade arrow** (Allen: "make the workshop->on the way->town->home without its background. - **2026-11-08: The anvil's shape cards show only the price, no stars; every picker card has a blue well with rays and halftone and a bigger item** (Allen: "This looks really ugly, and the stars don't really mean much", then "Both"). `Blueprints.difficulty` stays (the Showcase pull uses it) but is no longer shown. Group → user transfer isn't offered by Roblox, so the game stays with the group. Development stays in the Sell Swords! universe 10771581195. On launch day: Build a Bridge's items go off sale, the code takes the new ids, Allen publishes over its start place, then the page and the questionnaire. Ore luck, gems and coins are left out, because merging is the only luck (2026-10-25), gems are sold for Robux, and coins would be the right pick every time. - **2026-11-09: Showcase Stands boost one stat each, by the sword's metal: six families** (Allen: "Its metal family", then "just do 6 for now, maybe later we can add more"):
  - Customers 1%/point (cap 20%);
  - VIP 0.5 pp/point after the multipliers (cap +10 pp);
  - Mail: letters 1%/point sooner and a 1%/point gem chance (caps 30% / 33%);
  - Luck +0.01/point (cap +0.15);
  - Coins 0.5%/point (cap 8%);
  - Swift 0.5%/point walk speed (cap 12%).

  Points are the old pull x100. Crew, Forge, Cart and a separate Gems family are left out: they paid ~0 while the shop is short of customers. The stands' luck counts on every odds panel. Each pass stays well ahead of full stands. Claude's metal table and numbers; supersedes "every stand pulls customers" (2026-11-04). [[Sessions/2026-11-09#Showcase families built: six stats by metal]]
