[[Sessions/2026-11-10#Curled Flame: red and black like the reference]]
- **2026-11-10: Flame's flames run into the point** (Allen: "i wanted the etch to go all the way to the tip, not stop. i know the reference did, but that's unintentional"). They shrink with the width until the point is a sliver, then the band runs out to the tip. This replaces the solid colour over the point from the red-and-black pass. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Curled Flame: flames into the point]]
- **2026-11-10: S04 and Tidebreaker are held turned half a turn from the sheet** (Allen: Tidebreaker "should be rotated 180 degrees", S04 "should be reverse so the curve is outward"). Their edge and curve face away from the player; the rest keep the sheet's side. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#New silhouettes as real swords (in progress)]]
- **2026-11-10: Flame's flames are drawn in the etch colour, the edge keeps the blade's material** (Allen: "if i choose red, then the flames up top are the ones being drawn and red, and the bottom keeps its material"). This replaces the dark face with a coloured edge from the red-and-black pass. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Curled Flame: flames in the etch colour, metal along the edge]]
- **2026-11-10: White and black join the etch colours** (Allen: "there's no black or white etch colors"). Black is the one etch colour below full brightness; both sit under the Default button, so the panel keeps its width. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Etch colours: white and black]]
- **2026-11-10: Less bloom, and no weather brighter than 2.8** (Allen: "why does the map glow so much at certain times and places"). Bloom catches only what is well over white (Threshold 1.8, Size 24); Rainbow lights the map like Sunny, Golden Hour at 2.8. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Less glow on the paving and walls]]
- **2026-11-10: The roll's finishing chime rings only for a new metal** (Allen: "only chime if there's a new undiscovered one? i feel like the chime every time gets annoying"). It plays when a stand shows NEW (a metal's first roll); other rolls end without it. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Ore roll chime only for a new metal]]
- **2026-11-10: The Lucky Ore board follows Allen's mockup, and its multiplier is the true one** (Allen: "can you see if we can make it look more like this"; "lucky ore and then 1.3x rare ore luck seems a bit redundant"; "the ores you picked to show aren't even hard to find, they should be legendary ores"; "we don't need the 'every 100 pulls stats'"). A dark wood board with a gold edge: "Lucky Ore", one green line "N× rarer ore, more often" (Rare and better, with the pass over without, at the plot's luck: 1.5× on a fresh save, smaller as merges and rain add luck, since the pass adds a flat +0.5), three Legendary ingots, the table Current | With Lucky Ore with ▲/▼, and a full-width "Get Lucky Ore · price" button. No duration line (the pass is permanent) and no 100-pull count. Supersedes the 2026-11-07 Buy-headed column, its "›" arrows and the stamp. The board is 1.35× bigger so it reads over the ore stands (Allen: "make the board taller"). Claude's defaults: Diamond, Rainbow Alloy and Sunsteel; the multiplier rounded to 0.1; owned shows "Without" (struck) and "You". [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lucky Ore board redesign (dark wood, real multiplier, bigger)]]
- **2026-11-10: The book's Metals | Swords tabs are one pill, as the Gem Store's** (Allen: "make this this a pill selector just like how our shop and store has one", "too much top and bottom padding"). A 38 px dark pill across the page; the open tab is a copper half, the other clear; no orange borders or edge art. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Book tabs: a Metals | Swords pill]]
- **2026-11-10: The Shop's weather cards say how long a bought weather lasts** (Allen: "the weather purchases in shop down say how long they last"). "5 min" under each card's effect. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Weather cards say how long they last]]
- **2026-11-10: The Shop's Packs row and tab go once there's nothing in them** (Allen: "after buying the two packs, the top is empty now"). The Featured row hides with its last card; the Packs tab hides when the Everything Bundle is gone too. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Shop: no empty Packs row once both packs are bought]]
- **2026-11-10: The shop door bell is a short hand bell, quieter** (Allen: "im hearing some metal clanging sounds while just walking around", "i like hand bell, but it's a bit too long ... lower the volume too"). The cowbell-like teapot bell goes; the hand bell plays its first 1.1 s at half the volume. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Door bell sounds like clanging]]
- **2026-11-10: The offer card's Decline / More words are the same size as Accept's** (Allen: "the decline button is so much bigger, why?"). Both at 20 pt (14 on a phone); longer "More [n%] (L2)" shrinks to fit. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Offer card: Decline words as big as Accept's]]
- **2026-11-10: S05 keeps its open fork; a blade's hole may open out of its side** (Allen: "s05 held on hand is different than on display", then "Build fork support"). The traced S05 had lost its right prong (one spine and one edge per station). Its slot and the V between its prongs now share the blade's one hole, closed across the bridge between them; `fork = true` lets the V reach the blade's sides so the shorter prong narrows to nothing. Hilt tier colours on the new silhouettes stay as they are (the gallery's gold is the Blender model's own). Crownsplitter, Cometspine, S08 and Mothwing differ from their sheets too: not changed. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#S05 fork]]
- **2026-11-10: One upload script, always as the group; the old economy model is retired** (Allen: "work on all of them", after the repo audit). `Development/upload_asset.py` replaces the 14 per-folder upload copies, which still uploaded as user 14722022 (a group place can't load those). `Development/economy_model.py` is gone: the simulator is the one pacing model. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Repo audit fixes]]

- **2026-11-10: The bag holds 30 (60 with the Bigger Bag pass, 49 R$)** (Allen: "I want to cap player inventory sizes", then his picks: one shared count, each ingot 1, refuse and leave the item, a Robux pass, 30, "49 robux"). Only a chosen pick-up checks it; returns, put-backs and gifts still arrive past it. Supersedes "no limit on ingots" (2026-10-24, the Ingot Rack's "remove this limit") and "no limit for now" on carried swords (2026-10-17). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Bag cap and the Bigger Bag pass]]
- **2026-11-10: Lucky Ore is now Lucky Metal** (Allen: "change our game pass and mentions of Lucky ore to lucky metal, feels like it makes more sense"). The pass (Swordsmith Tycoon 2075536519) and every player-facing string; the code keys (`LuckyOre`, `Store.LUCKY_ORE`, `Pass_LuckyOre`, `WorldStore.LuckyOre`) keep their names. The Ore Chances board keeps its name. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lucky Ore renamed Lucky Metal]]
- **2026-11-10: Bigger Bag is in the Everything Bundle; the bundle stays 599 R$** (Allen: "Add it to the bundle"). Nine passes, 1,241 R$ apart. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Bag cap and the Bigger Bag pass]]
- **2026-11-10: The Auto Ore Roller has no spot by the lever before it is bought** (Allen: "auto ore roller buy prompt but nothing physical there"; a sign post was tried, then "actually just remove it. dont need it": the sign and the Buy prompt). Bought from the Shop and the Auto-Roll bar's lock; the lever hand villager still appears once owned. Supersedes the 2026-10-27 "popup with a lock icon" near the roller as a world prompt. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Auto Ore Roller: no spot before it's bought]]
- **2026-11-10: The thumbnail staging's WallRack4 shows three katanas: Sapphire, Damascus, Obsidian** (Allen: "i want al 3 swords to be katanas. give them high contrast with the wall and shelves. 1 of them can be damascus"). Blue Steel was tried on top and read pale on the beige wall. Edit-only staging; the game's racks are unchanged. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Thumbnail staging: WallRack4 katanas]]
- **2026-11-10: Lucky Metal is a one-time developer product, not a pass** (Allen: "just do one-time product then makes no difference"). Product 3730271492 at 149 R$ (managed pricing on); the pass 2075536519 is off sale. A pass can be bought on the experience's Store tab where the game can't check `PolicyService`; the product's receipt is refused where paid random items are restricted. It left the Everything Bundle (now 8 passes, still 599 R$; Claude's consequence of the change, not asked). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Release gates: the fixes]]
- **2026-11-10: The Dev panel stays live for goku19876 only** (Allen: "stays live for my username only goku19876"; `Admins.USERS` already had only 14722022). Its coins and gems no longer count as earned, so they stay off the leaderboards.
- **2026-11-10: Musashi off for launch** (Allen: "turn musashi off"). `Shared/Musashi.ENABLED = false`.
- **2026-11-10: Swordsmith Tycoon is public, server size 6** (Allen: "its public", "its set to 6").
- **2026-11-10: Live leaderboards start fresh on `_v2`; Studio writes `_Studio_v1`** (Allen: "Yes").
- **2026-11-10: The customers count's max updates within a second of buying a bay or hiring the Promoter** (Allen: "when i buy the bays for +1 customer at a time, my 0/2 doesnt increase"). It waited for the next customer; a bug fix, rules unchanged. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Customers count follows a bay at once]]
- **2026-11-10: Purchase event, Shop and SwordChain funnels** (Allen: "Add them"). Every Robux buy in the game logs Purchase (Robux, item, Pass/Product, time played); the Shop funnel counts openings → Buy pressed → bought; SwordChain has one step per main-chain sword, Wakizashi → Seraph, big swords left out. [[Development/Analytics Funnels]]
- **2026-11-10: A walk-in that comes due while the shop is full waits and comes in as soon as a shopper leaves** (Allen: "Yes, fix it"). It was dropped, so a full shop lost a whole walk-in gap. The next gap starts once they're sent. The customer system otherwise stays the Create Pottery kind (offers on crafted swords), not Retail Tycoon's queues and stocking. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#A walk-in due while the shop is full waits for room]]
- **2026-11-10: The Longsword to the Gladius cost x2 a step: 7,200 / 14,400 / 29,000 / 58,000 / 115,000** (Allen: "Yes, build Even"; were 13,300 / 33,800 / 36,400 / 65,000 / 78,000). The same coins up to the Zanbatō (223,600 against 226,500); in the sim the new swords come evenly spaced and the Zanbatō and Seraph move under 2%. The "Value" set (cheap swords, a 248,000 Zanbatō) was not picked: it uses the five swords up by 3:20 for a casual player. Supersedes those five prices of 2026-11-09's x1.3. [[Design/Sword Chain Pricing — 2026-11-10]]
- **2026-11-10: The third etch is Lightning; Fracture is retired** (Allen: "i want to replace the fracture design", "don't we have a lightning etch already?", "yes, but call it Lightning. and fix it as you suggested"). The release set is Flame, Wind, Lightning. Lightning is the retired Stormroot made bolder (thicker toward the tip, longer forks: Claude's numbers) and takes Fracture's pad, 52,000 price and +20%. The 45 concepts in `output/etch-concepts` stay ideas (the gem etches of 2026-11-09 are still open). Supersedes 2026-11-01's pick of Fracture as the third. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lightning etch replaces Fracture]]
- **2026-11-10: The Katana and the Sword Crafter are the main attractions: both inside two sessions** (Allen: "Ideally katana and sword crafter are main attractions, they should get it in two sessions or less", with "average tycoon playtime of 10-15 per day"). So both by 20-30 min of casual play; replaces "affordable by ~15 min" (2026-11-02). Prices simmed, none built yet. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Katana and Sword Crafter inside two sessions (target set, prices simmed, nothing built)]]
- **2026-11-10: The Sword Crafter costs 1,400** (Allen picked "2", Crafter 1,400 only, over Claude's pick of Crafter 1,000 + Katana 900). Was 2,000 since 2026-11-02. Sim: casual players get him at 19 min instead of 28; both attractions inside two 12.5-min sessions for 97% (two 10-min sessions 66%). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Sword Crafter 1,400 built]]
- **2026-11-10: The coin tick plays low and nearly flat** (Allen: "not a fan of the high pitched sound when the coins collect into your inventory"). Pitch 0.72 rising 0.015 a coin, was 1.0 rising 0.04. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Coin tick lower and flatter]]
- **2026-11-10: Faster Heating I always stands** (Allen: "i also dont see furnace speed increase ... having the 1st furnace already available", then "Yes, always show"). Outside the 3-pad cap like the Delivery Cart; the cap hid it behind five cheaper pads. Polish Boost stays after Polishing Station 2. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Faster Heating I always stands]]
- **2026-11-10: The first pull after the tutorial brings a Legendary for sure** (Allen: "shouldnt we just always give them an epic or something so we know what they can buy after the tutorial for sure ... that way all players are still starting equal", then "Legendary"). One Mythril on the first stand; the rest of the pull and later pulls at the usual odds (Claude's defaults). Replaces beginner's luck (2026-11-01). Sim: casual Katana 14.4 min, Sword Crafter 16.9. Pending: in the sim, written but not yet in the place (Studio was in Play). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Guaranteed Mythril on the first pull after the tutorial (sim done; the game edit is pending, Studio was in Play)]]
- **2026-11-10: After the tutorial, a new offer you're too far to see spotlights the customers row for 2 s** (Allen: "if the player isn't in the store or close enough to accept an offer, can we spotlight to this for a moment"). "Too far" = past the offer card's draw distance (35 studs from the camera); 2 s is Claude's default. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Far offers spotlight the customers row]]
- **2026-11-10: The guaranteed Mythril is in the place** (was pending in the entry above; Allen: "Play stopped, apply"). `ForgeService` `FIRST_PULL_METAL`; beginner's luck removed. Not played. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Guaranteed Mythril built in ForgeService]]
- **2026-11-10: The far-offer spotlight carries a line: "You have X customers and 1 made an offer!"** (Allen: "you only spotlighted but didnt explain what it means"). On the guide line, for the same 2 s. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Far offers spotlight the customers row]]
- **2026-11-10: The Lucky Metal board shows five Legendary ingots without glows, and its buy button is wider** (Allen: "use 5 ingots here in the row instead of 3. remove the glows", "make the padding on left and right smaller"). Bloodmoon and Mythril added (Claude's pick). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lucky Metal board: five ingots, no glows, wider button]]
- **2026-11-10: The Lucky Metal board's ingots are Bloodmoon, Oni Steel, Soulfire, Doppler, Dragonsteel; its buy button is narrower (0.72) and its title just text** (Allen's picks; supersedes the wider button and Claude's ingot pick from earlier today). Button and title pending. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lucky Metal board: five ingots, no glows, wider button]]
- **2026-11-10: The Lucky Metal odds table is square with no outline** (Allen: "set this border radius to 0 so its a rectangular table, remove the red/orange outline around"); the button (0.72) and text-only title are now in. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lucky Metal board: five ingots, no glows, wider button]]
- **2026-11-10: The Lucky Metal board's column header says "Lucky Metal", not "With Lucky Metal"** (Allen). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Lucky Metal board: five ingots, no glows, wider button]]
- **2026-11-10: The coin tick is sound 134583420216867** (Allen: "use this coin sound to replace the current one"); was 116120982042508. Same volume and pitch. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#New coin tick sound]]
- **2026-11-10: The coin tick is 330274138 ("Coin Drop")** (Allen: "actually this one"); supersedes 134583420216867 from minutes before. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#New coin tick sound]]
- **2026-11-10: Each landing coin plays its own Coin Drop, at a random pitch 0.9-1.1, 0.09 s apart** (Allen: "sound like multiple coin drops when the coins tween into it"). Supersedes the low, flat 0.72 + 0.015 tick of earlier today. Pitch range and spacing are Claude's. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#New coin tick sound]]
- **2026-11-10: The upgrade-bought sound is 133292918309565 ("buy")** (Allen); was 80736831159506. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#New upgrade sound]]
- **2026-11-10: Every upgrade bought plays the upgrade sound, pad or not** (Allen: "buying ore slot multiple times in a row only triggers 1 sound"). A bug fix: the sound was tied to the pad still existing. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Every upgrade bought chimes]]
- **2026-11-10: The offer card's Accept / More buttons are 38 px tall, the card 120 px** (Allen: "less padding above and below so its skinner"); were 50 and 132; the More words keep a 20 px box (padding 9 / 9) so they stay Accept's size. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Slimmer offer buttons]]
- **2026-11-10: The far-offer spotlight is a one-off hint: once per player, ever** (Allen: "once it happens it cant happen again its a one-off hint to show players what the ui means"). Saved as funnel id 16. Supersedes "every far offer" from earlier today. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Far-offer hint once ever; VIP bars edge to edge]]
- **2026-11-10: The VIP entrance's black bars run edge to edge on phones** (Allen: "black bars when vip entrance on mobile"): CutsceneGui ignores the safe area. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Far-offer hint once ever; VIP bars edge to edge]]

- **2026-11-10: Rainbow Alloy uses Allen's gradient picture** (Allen: "i want to replace our current rainbow metal with this one"). Mirrored both ways into a wrapping tile. Supersedes the original spectrum-band texture (2026-10-23). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Rainbow Alloy: Allen's gradient]]

- **2026-11-10: More Sales, a 5th gem upgrade: walk-ins may pass on a sword** (Allen: "a player can then upgrade that stat so customers are more likely to buy something"; named "More Sales"). A walk-in offers at 60% (Lv. 0) to 90% (Lv. 5), +10% for a polished sword and +10% for an etched one; on a no they try one other free sword, then leave. The tutorial's first two sales and VIPs always offer. 15 / 25 / 40 / 65 / 100 gems. Levels and prices are Claude's. Supersedes "every walk-in with a free sword makes an offer". [[Sessions/2026-11-10#More Sales: customers may pass on a sword]]

- **2026-11-10: Secret metals move: a moving texture plus a particle trickle, held swords too; the lineup becomes Galaxy, Lava Lamp and Glitch** (Allen: "for the secret metals i feel like they're kinda bland... maybe we can add some type of special effect or animated textures"; picked Moving texture + particles, "Yes, on held swords too", and Galaxy, Lava Lamp, Glitch from Claude's options). Each Secret metal gets a second texture layer on the blade that scrolls or jumps, and a few particles; drawn by the client only near you, at most 6 swords at once. No glow lights (phones). Watermelon, Cardboard and Rubber Duck leave the roll. Held Secrets move on purpose: an exception to the 2026-11-05 no-moving-glint-on-held-swords rule, which stays for the polish glint. Not built. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Secret metals: moving textures and a new lineup]]
- **2026-11-10: Only VIPs talk in the shop** (Allen: "normal customers just come in browse, make an offer or leave"). Regular walk-ins show no speech bubbles; VIPs keep every line. Supersedes the regulars' lines (Features and fixes 2026-10-29, "Add more NPC dialogues") and the "pass" line added earlier today for them. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Only VIPs talk]]

- **2026-11-10: A group reward and codes** (Allen: "a reward for joining the group and a codes section to input codes for free things"). Joining the Swordsmith Tycoon group (336242048) gives **15 gems once**; codes typed in the Daily Reward window (under the days: Allen "actually you can just put them in the daily rewards window", after first asking for the top of Settings) give their reward once each per save, the first `SWORDS` = **500 coins** (Allen: "15", "coins, gives 500 coins"). Codes live on the server only. The code's name is Claude's. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Group reward and codes]]

- **2026-11-10: Watermelon and Rubber Duck become Epic, Cardboard Rare** (Allen: "make watermelon an epic, cardboard a rare, and duck sword an epic"). They stay rollable with their looks; Secret is Galaxy, Lava Lamp and Glitch only. Showcase families for the three new ones (Galaxy Mail, Lava Lamp Coins, Glitch Swift) are Claude's. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Moving Secret metals built]]

- **2026-11-10: Galaxy and Lava Lamp move in layers, not one sliding sheet** (Allen: "both galaxy and lava lamp just shift up"): stars twinkle in two layers with a faint drift; wax blobs rise at different speeds, sway, and one layer sinks. Claude's numbers. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Secret metals: layered motion]]

- **2026-11-10: The Cashier's slider hint says what happens under the pick** (Allen: "this is confusing what does it mean"): "He takes every offer" at the bottom step, "Under +N%: he says no" above it. Behaviour unchanged (he declines, the customer may raise). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Cashier: slider hint and his gate]]

- **2026-11-10: The Cashier pad stands outside the pad cap** (Allen: "i already had the sword crafter and the upgraded store, yet still couldnt buy it"): once the Upgraded Store and Sword Crafter are bought, like the Stocker and Faster Heating I. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Cashier pad always stands]]

- **2026-11-10: The Shop Promoter pad stands outside the pad cap too** (Allen: "Yes, Promoter too"), once the Sword Crafter is bought. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Cashier pad always stands]]

- **2026-11-10: Metal tiers re-sorted to 8 / 8 / 9 / 9 / 8 / 3** (Allen: "we have more legendary metals than we do epic or uncommon or rare"; picked Claude's table). Steel, Bronze → Common; Blue Steel, Silver, Amethyst, Damascus, Sapphire → Uncommon; Ruby, Emerald, Gold, Runestone → Rare; Mythril, Starmetal, Sunsteel, Prism, Raijin → Epic. Supersedes the 2026-11-04 tiers. Owned swords of moved metals are worth less (accepted). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Metal tiers re-sorted 8 / 8 / 9 / 9 / 8 / 3]]

- **2026-11-10: The pad cap is 4** (Allen: "can we make the 3-pad to 4-pads?"; was 3 since 2026-10-30, "Keep the cap"): four new chains' cheapest pads stand at once. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Pad cap 4]]

- **2026-11-10: The Roll prompt shows at the ore lever's base, not the red knob** (Allen: "on the base of the ore roller instead of the red lever"). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Roll prompt at the lever's base]]

- **2026-11-10: A sword put on a Showcase Stand floats what it adds** (Allen: "can we have it emit a text that explains what is boosted"): "+4% coins", the family's word, the amount its total actually gained; "(max)" at the cap, "Max coins" when full. Once per placing, not on join. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Showcase stand says what it adds]]

- **2026-11-10: The crew window has role tabs, like the Shop's** (Allen: "add a tab to the crew menu similar to the shop, so that players can quickly go to their selected crew"): Crafters, Stockers, Cashiers, Promoters, Etchers on the top border scroll the roster to that section. Reverses "no tabs" (2026-10-29), which was about tabs that switched pages. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Crew role tabs; F at a crew member opens their page]]

- **2026-11-10: F at a crew member opens their page instead of the level-up confirm** (Allen: "make it open up to that Crew member's profile/page. because they can still upgrade from that page" ... "but keep the gem cost on it"): the prompt still shows the level and gem price. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Crew role tabs; F at a crew member opens their page]]

- **2026-11-10: The Showcase Stand's "+N%" pop stays 2.5 s** (Allen: "it fades away too quick. make it last like 2.5 seconds"; was 1.8 s). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Showcase pop lasts 2.5 s]]

- **2026-11-10: The mail closes as soon as you press Send** (Allen: "it should just close the window after"), before the server answers; a failed send still toasts. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Mail shuts on Send]]

- **2026-11-10: Gifts from the Shop** (Allen: "add a gift button so players can gift their friends a gamepass or currency or whatever it is from the shop"). Claude's calls: a gift button on each giftable card, to anyone in the server (friends first), not to offline friends; a pass can't be bought for someone else, so each gift is its own developer product at the item's price and a gifted pass is saved on the friend; gifts to someone who left before the receipt wait in an inbox for their next load; not giftable: the Starter Pack, Lucky Metal and weather; a pass they own (or a bundle holding one) can't be gifted to them; the button is blue with the Red Gift icon. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Gifts from the Shop]]

- **2026-11-10: The gift button is as tall as Buy** (Allen: "either increase the gift button size or decrease the height of the buy button"): the gift grew wider (62 × 42) so its edge draws at full size; Buy keeps its height. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Gift button as tall as Buy]]

- **2026-11-10: A tap beside any menu closes it** (Allen: "i should be able to click off to the side outside of the shop or whatever menu im on to close ... it helps a lot for mobile players"): every dimmed window and confirm, through `UiFx.tapOutside`. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Tap beside a menu to close it]]

- **2026-11-10: The anvil strikes on a tap anywhere; the strike circle is gone** (Allen: "lets just allow players to tap anywhere on the screen to progress it"; on a phone the circle sat where thumbs don't reach). The anvil camera is 0.8 as far and pans so the anvil sits lower on the screen (Allen: "shift the camera so the anvil is lower to the screen and zoom in a bit more"). Supersedes "hit every circle" (2026-10-19). [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Anvil: tap anywhere, camera lower and closer]]

- **2026-11-10: A walnut look for the Shop and Crew, for review** (Allen: "a warmer, quieter frame that gives the game's colorful icons, character portraits, and item art room to stand out"): dark walnut windows `#322820`, walnut cards `#493A30`, cream text `#F7EBD8`, brass `#E2B66B` for the lit tab and selection (working crew outlined brass, was green), muted green `#A8C76B` for buy/hire/level buttons (was lime 115,220,35, with dark words, no outline), a neutral X (was red), the seigaiha only near the header, thinner half-clear outlines on text inside panels. Ordinary offers are walnut cards with the item's colour on a tile behind its icon; featured packs keep their bright art. Crew cards carry a Working / Resting / For hire / Locked badge, an "Anvil 2 · Forging" status line ("Waiting for ingots" amber), and the work tick box became a worded Rest / Put to work button. The Shop window is 396 tall (was 456) so it fits a 338-px phone. Supersedes for these two windows the 2026-10-28 brown menu gradient, lime buttons, red X and the green working outline; other windows keep them until Allen picks this direction. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Walnut pass: Shop and Crew (first look, for review)]]

## Forge Lab prototype (2026-11-11)

- Three anvil modes side by side (Original, Enhanced, Interactive) behind a dev-only switch, so the feel can be compared before choosing; Original stays the shipped game until a decision. Players outside Studio and the Admins list always get Original.
- All modes keep the 10-strike recipe and the server check; Interactive's finisher lands the last 3 strikes at once.
- Misses never stall: an off-target tap still strikes, more weakly.

## Forge Lab refinements (2026-11-11)

- The last blow is the reveal: no forced cooling or camera wait after it; the blade cools to steel on the hilt seat while play goes on. Rare metals get a slightly stronger, slightly longer ending, never a wait.
- Camera framing is compared, not chosen: Fixed three-quarter vs Dynamic (overhead for shaping, one swing to a close three-quarter for the last blow).
- Not assumed: that Interactive beats a polished Enhanced. Allen picks after playtesting the five versions.

## Forge Lab versions are picked directly (2026-11-11)

- Five version buttons replace mode buttons plus a camera toggle, and a press restarts the blade on the anvil, because a mode that only applied to the next ingot made the versions hard to tell apart. A banner names the version being forged.
- Enhanced hits were made stronger (shake 1.0 degree, hitstop 70 ms) so the feel difference from Original can be judged at all; still inside the prototype's limits (blade never hidden, hitstop 40-70 ms).

## Forge Lab third pass (2026-11-11)

- In the lab modes the blade lies still and the hammer travels to it, and each blow reshapes most where it lands. Original keeps its sliding blade as the baseline.
- The camera is its own setting (A Overhead, B Hybrid 70 degrees, C Three-quarter), fixed during forging, so framing can be judged apart from the mechanic. The Dynamic camera is dropped.
- Interactive is 4 targeted taps (one glowing spot on the metal at a time) plus one timed finisher. Each tap lands several recipe strikes, so the server still sees the same 10-strike state. Combo and Refining phase removed.
- Long blades are drawn smaller on the anvil (at most 1.15 anvil faces) rather than hanging far past it.

## Live error report, second pass (2026-11-11)

- The raised shop gets an invisible ramp at the door rather than shrinking the plinth or resizing friend-look NPCs: players' friends keep their real avatar proportions, and small bodies can't step the 0.86-stud ledge.
- NpcWalk walks to a nearby path point when the straight line past it would brush something, instead of always skipping points within 1.5 studs.

## Stocker bumping (2026-11-11)

- NPC routes keep their room from tabletops and shelves too, not only from legs and walls. NPCs still pass through those parts physically (NpcPass), so none get stuck standing on a table. Customers stand a little further back from the display tables.
- A route shortcut always keeps body room (1.1 studs), however tight the points it skips.

## Forge Lab held hammer (2026-11-11)

- Trying a first-person held hammer as a toggle (`Arm`), not a replacement: the handle points at the camera, a gloved hand and forearm come in from the bottom-right, and the hammer stays in hand between blows. The floating hammer that appears per strike stays available for comparison. Allen's pick so far is Enhanced with camera A.

## Dev panel Players list (2026-11-11)

- Join count is a saved number (`joins`, +1 per save load), not read from analytics: Allen sees it in game for the players in his server. Playtime reuses the leaderboard's saved `played`. Admin-only, through the Dev panel; old saves count joins from this change on.

## Anvil: the Forge Lab's Enhanced ships (2026-11-11)

- Every player now forges with the Enhanced feel and Original's overhead camera, and no held arm: a still blade, the hammer travelling to each spot, local deformation, hitstop, heat colours, and the last blow as the reveal. Allen compared the lab's versions and picked this one ("just keep the enhanced original view with no arms").
- Interactive (targets and timed finisher), cameras B and C, and the held arm are dropped, not hidden; they are in Legacy backups if wanted again.

## Anvil hammer modelled in Blender (2026-11-11)

- The swing hammer is a Blender cross-peen hammer with the stations' soft bevels (asset 89166524834515, template `ReplicatedStorage.AnvilLook.Hammer`), replacing the part-built head, handle and striking face.

## Anvil hammer head as one solid (2026-11-11)

- The hammer's steel head is one lofted solid with bevelled edges (face, neck, cheek and peen flowing into each other), not overlapping blocks. Asset 114342383380193.

## Hearth stone texture (2026-11-11)

- Every hearth uses Cobblestone + PaintStoneWall (colour 0.388/0.373/0.384), the look Allen set on `Anvil2.Hearth`. A part-built rim to hide the stretched sides was tried and dropped; the sides stay as they are.

## Wooden tubs and stumps, opaque water (2026-11-11)

- Quench tubs, polish buckets and hammer stumps use the Wood material; their water is opaque with no reflection (Transparency 0, Reflectance 0).

## Lucky Rolls sold on Lucky Metal's board (2026-11-11)

Allen: "re-design this to include the lucky metal pack purchases", then "Build it" on Claude's mockup. The board sells Lucky Metal ("Pass"; "Forever" at first, Allen: "change forever to \"Pass\"") and the three Lucky Rolls packs in one row; one "Lucky" column covers both (each adds one merge); owners of Lucky Metal see a "+ Roll" column. [[Sessions/2026-11-11#Lucky Rolls on Lucky Metal's board]]

## Invite friends: gems for both, and a friends coin boost (2026-11-11)

Allen: "i want an add friend bonus feature to our game. so players can invite friends to get a reward." He picked both options Claude offered: an invite reward and a friends boost, with gems as the reward.
- **Invite reward:** a top bar Invite button opens Roblox's invite window. A player whose **first ever join** comes from someone's invite (`GetJoinData().ReferredByPlayerId`) gets 25 gems, and the inviter gets 25 too, for up to 10 invited players. Nothing is paid for sending an invite (Roblox can't confirm a send, and many kids' accounts can't invite: the button hides for them).
- **Friends boost:** +5% coins for each Roblox friend in the server, up to +15%, through `Store.coinBoost` like the passes.
- Claude's defaults: 25 gems and the 10-player cap; an inviter who isn't in the same server when the friend arrives gets nothing (rare: an invite join lands in the inviter's server when there's room); the Add Player icon from the pack. [[Sessions/2026-11-11#Invite friends and the friends boost]]

- **2026-11-11: Top bar order Settings, Invite, Gift** (Allen: "can we have the order at the top be settings, invite friends, gift"); Boosts after them (Claude's default). [[Sessions/2026-11-11#Top bar order: Settings, Invite, Gift]]

## Lucky Metal's board spins its ingots (2026-11-11)

Allen: "as if its a slot machine every few seconds its rolling a new set of random legendary/secret/epic ores", then "build it". Every 4 s, five different Epic / Legendary / Secret ingots, weighted 45 / 45 / 10 (Claude's mix, not the real odds; the table shows those). [[Sessions/2026-11-11#Lucky Metal's board: a slot-machine ingot reel]]

## Ingot reel: 15 s, scrolling, column by column (2026-11-11)

Allen: "happens way too frequently, lets do every 15 seconds. also any way for them to actually spin down as if its a slot", "make the spin happen column by column". Each column starts as the one before it starts landing (Claude's reading of "column by column", so a full roll takes ~3.6 s). [[Sessions/2026-11-11#Ingot reel: every 15 s, real slot spin, column by column]]

Ingot reel every 60 s (2026-11-11, Allen: "lets have it every 1 minute"; was 15 s).

## 2x Coins replaces 50% More Coins (2026-11-11)

Allen: "would 2x coins be a better look than saying 50% more coins? ... i feel like 2x is more catchy and influencing", then "lets set it to 2x". The pass doubles coins (`Store.MORE_COINS` 2, was 1.5), named "2x Coins", 349 R$ (was 249; Claude's price), its gift the same. Supersedes the 2026-10-27 walkthrough's "2× Coins dropped for 50% More Coins" and the 2026-11-11 pricing pass's 249. Cost, stated once: the paying efficient player's Seraph comes at 4:12 instead of 5:25 (free 7:20). [[Design/Monetization Plan#2x Coins (2026-11-11)]]

## 2x Gems replaces 50% More Gems (2026-11-11)

Allen: "Yes, 2x Gems" (after 2x Coins). `Store.MORE_GEMS` 2 (was 1.5), named "2x Gems", 199 R$ (was 149; Claude's price), its gift the same. [[Design/Monetization Plan#2x Gems (2026-11-11)]]

## Visibility picks (2026-11-11)

From [[Design/Monetization Visibility Review — 2026-11-11]] (Allen: "I like 5 ...", "3. yes the + buttons would be good", "we could experiment with a starter pack hud button ... but still wait until after tutorial"):
- **A gold chat line when someone in the server buys** a pass, a bundle, the Starter Pack or Lucky Metal, or sends a gift. Currency packs, Lucky Rolls, the Daily Deal and weather stay quiet (Claude's line: no spending status; weather names its buyer already).
- **+ on the coin and gem counters** open the Shop at Currency, after the lessons.
- **A Starter Pack HUD button** under Mail, after the lessons, until owned; Roblox's window opens straight away. An experiment. Narrows 2026-10-31's "the Starter Pack is offered by the Shop button's dot, no popup": still no popup.
- Free gift card: mockup shown, not decided. Weather bell: approved, not built. [[Sessions/2026-11-11#Visibility picks: + buttons, chat line, Starter Pack button, free gift mockup]]

## The daily reward lives in the Shop (2026-11-12)

Allen: "lets try that" (the daily reward as a Free Gift card at the top of the Shop, the join popup gone; [[Design/Monetization Visibility Review — 2026-11-11]] proposals 1-2). The daily window no longer opens on join and the top bar gift loses its red circle; the Shop button's dot shows while today's gift waits and stays until it is claimed. Same rewards and streak. The top bar gift still opens the daily window (codes, the group reward). Supersedes 2026-10-24's red circle and the join popup (Refinements 2026-10-30). Also: the counters' + is a plain white + with a black outline (Allen: "just use the + with no green background"). [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Daily reward in the Shop; a plain + on the counters]]

## Lucky Rolls: one Shop card (2026-11-12)

Allen: "its one card where u can select the denomination" (with a reference). The Shop's three Lucky Rolls cards are one card: ×5 / ×15 / ×30 chips, Buy with the picked price, and Odds. Buy still opens the odds panel before Roblox's prompt (paid random items). [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Lucky Rolls: one Shop card with size chips]]

## The Free Gift card: half width, DAILY, the daily tiles (2026-11-12)

Allen: half width with the gems 4 over 3, no "Free Gift" title, Claim under the gift, smaller gems, "DAILY" on the border over the gift, the daily window's effects. It sits beside the Starter Pack in a two-across Packs row. The old daily window is REWARDS (codes and the group only): Allen, "we also now have 2 daily rewards pages". [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Free Gift card: half width, DAILY, the daily window's tiles and effects]]

## Lucky Rolls sit in the Packs row (2026-11-12)

Allen: "move the lucky rolls up into the now blank space": the Lucky Rolls card is the fourth card of the two-across Packs row (beside the Automation Pack), laid out like its neighbours. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Lucky Rolls in the Packs row]]

## The counters' + is tapped on the icon only (2026-11-12)

Allen: "make sure it only opens shop when players intentionally hit the coins+/gems+ not when they click nearby it". The tap area is the coin or gem icon and its +, not the whole counter row. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#The counters' + opens the Shop only from the icon]]

## The Starter Pack button sits above Mail (2026-11-12)

Allen: "the starterpack also covers the area where the quests are. move it above the mail icon". [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Starter Pack button above Mail]]

## Lucky Metal board's ingot reel is off for now (2026-11-12)

Allen: "lets remove the ore rolling animation on the lucky metal board for now, its distracting." `WorldStoreController.REEL_ON` false; the ingots stand still. Supersedes the 2026-11-11 reel (every 60 s) until it's switched back. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Lucky Metal board: the ingot reel off]]

## Roblox Journeys alongside funnels (2026-11-12)

Allen: "Build all three". Three journeys show which way players go, where funnels only count how many get through: BuyOrder (what kind of pad they buy, in order), SwordFate (what happens to each sword they forge) and AfterTutorial (the first five new things after the tutorial). Onboarding stays a funnel (the tutorial is linear) and Robux paths stay PassPrompt's CF3. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Roblox Journeys: three built]]

## The first merge comes about 35 minutes in (2026-11-12)

Allen: "make the first merge cheaper". Supersedes "first merge about 2 hours in" (2026-10-28) for the first cycle only: live, 2 of 385 players reached Ore Slot 6 and 12 have played an hour. Ore Slot 5 costs 3,000 and Ore Slot 6 6,000 before any merge (were 9,800 and 26,000); after a merge they cost what they did (×1.5 per merge from 9,800 and 26,000). The merge pad reads "Merge" with the Upgrade icon instead of "Merge Ore Slots". [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Cheaper first merge; the pad says "Merge" and the Upgrade icon]]

## The sim's live player works mail, etches and forges at 97% (2026-11-12)

Allen: "are there updates to our simulation based on how the players decide to play." From the 2026-11-12 analytics: live players fill 56% of mail orders (the casual type 10%), etch once they own the table (casual never) and forge at 97.1% on average (every type forged at 100). The live type takes all three from `live.json`; `forge` is a new player trait, 100 for the other types. Its buy order and offer habits were rebuilt from 2026-11-12 alone (after the Machete left the chain); slack stays 1.6. Haggling isn't changed: live players decline offers above value, which the sim's rule doesn't model. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Analytics: progression pace and the simulator]]

## FirstPull: why a new player never pulls the lever (2026-11-12)

Allen: "Record why the first pull does not happen" (proposal 3 of [[Design/Drop-off Fixes — 2026-11-12]]; 1, 2 and 4-7 not picked yet). One event per visit of a save that hasn't pulled: Pulled, or on leaving Let go early / In reach / Never in reach / Loading, with the early let-goes and the closest distance. Logged on the server from the lever's hold events and a once-a-second distance check, so no new remote. The join-to-streamed time from the proposal isn't added: LoadTime already covers it. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#FirstPull logging]]

- **2026-11-12: Swing2 and LeverRatchet go up as the group too** (Allen: "Yes, fix both", after the live error read). They're re-uploaded as group 336242048 (89810600943945, 81638967385735). This completes "Sounds go up as the group, not with a universe grant" (2026-11-11) and supersedes the 2026-11-08 universe grants for these two. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Fixed: double-tap starts and the two private sounds]]

- **2026-11-12: Crew levels a bit slower: Lv. 3–5 at 40 / 100 / 225 swords** (was 30 / 70 / 150; Allen: "can we slowdown the crew level up just a bit", the top live player had four crew at Lv. 5 by 5.5 h). Lv. 2 stays at 10, staff ×2.5, gem prices unchanged. A save from before keeps each member's level (the extra work credited). Amount picked by Claude. Supersedes the thresholds in "Employees max at Lv. 5, after several hours of work". [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Crew levels slowed a bit]]

## The hot blade breathes, no timing yet (2026-11-12)

Allen: "glow only". The hot metal on the anvil pulses (quick rise, slow fade, 1.5 s, down to 82% bright) for atmosphere only. A timed "PERFECT" strike (the pulse peak as the cue, perfect = 2 strikes, a fixed ±90 ms window that Faster Strikes doesn't shrink) was discussed and not built. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Hot blade breathes on the anvil]]

## The hot blade's outline is orange; no Neon (2026-11-12)

Allen tried Neon hot metal with a small orange light and said "nevermind"; the outline goes from cream to bright orange (255, 140, 40) instead. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Hot blade outline orange]]

## Notes 11-12-26 answers (2026-11-12, decided, not built)

Allen answered the organized [[Notes 11-12-26]]. Details and open questions are in the note. **Built the same day (Edit, not played):** Damascus and Sapphire Epic, Swift Boots 19 R$, no lever villager, the weather line, the hide arrow, the welcome-back window ([[Sessions/2026-11-12#Notes 11-12-26: first five builds]]).

- **Damascus and Sapphire back to Epic** ("Move damscus and saphire to Epic."), undoing that part of the 2026-11-10 re-tier.
- **Swift Boots 19 R$** ("Set it to 19 robux"), from 99; the cheapest pass.
- **No lever villager:** the Auto Ore Roller's lever swings by itself while Auto rolls ("just remove the lever villager").
- **Weather shows as a line:** name, bonus and time left beside the icon; a tap on the icon hides or shows it. Replaces the 2026-10-28 icon-only look.
- **A < / > arrow hides Book, Shop, Crew and Upgrades** ("only hide the book/shop/crew/upgrades").
- **Shop and Crew tabs on the left, plus a Featured tab;** one long list, a tab scrolls to its section. Featured = the Starter Pack and the Everything Bundle (Allen: "Featured i want everything pack and the starterpack"). Built the same day.
- **Welcome-back window** on join for away earnings.
- **Chat line for sold Legendary+ swords only;** none for crafting. At most one a minute per seller (Allen: "One per minute"). Built the same day.
- **Away earnings come from the swords on display** being sold while away, plus a smaller share of recent income (replaces the 2026-11-11 income-only model).
- **A 2x away earnings product** ("its a popular purchase in Tycoons"), overriding the Don't list line "No product boosts away earnings".
- **Giving metals to players in the server is allowed** ("you're not getting anything yourself"), with a limit; changes "no player trading in v1" for one-way gifts.
- **Friends can be allowed to work your shop, never buy** ("only a positive, not a negative"); changes the 2026-10-21 rule "visitors can walk into other plots but not use anything" for allowed friends.
- **Later:** shop star rating ("we need to refine"), reward boosts (Lucky Rolls, weather, gem speed levels), cosmetics ("needs more refinement in the models and placements").

## Playtest fixes to the Notes 11-12-26 builds (2026-11-12)

Allen, after playing them: "the < close button is too large. make it much much smaller and closer to icons but not touching them"; "make the weather texts a bit smaller like 25% smaller"; "starterpack lets have the price on it ... make it tween a bit like the shop does occassionally"; "shop ui is fucked, there's overlaps everywhere and not enough spacing between things".

- **The hide arrow is a small chevron** just right of SHOP / UPGRADES, with a larger invisible tap area.
- **The weather line is 25% smaller.**
- **Shop cards per row** (Claude's fix for the overlaps, which came from the left tabs narrowing the list): Packs one a row, passes two a row (was three, Allen's 2026-11-09 pick for the wider list), currency three a row (was four), 18 px between cards.
- **The STARTER button shows its live price** on a dark pill and hops like the Shop icon.
- **Review fixes:** a guide spotlight opens a folded menu; a Dev Reset clears the welcome-back window's coins.
- **Open:** the Starter Pack's 24 h countdown and "limited time offer" (what happens at zero), 10,000 coins and more rewards, and rotating packs on the button every minute; then a bright, colourful UI style overhaul. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Playtest fixes: arrow, weather, Shop spacing, Starter price]]

## Starter Pack: limited for 24 hours, then gone for good (2026-11-12, decided, not built)

Allen: "it should be gone for good." The Starter Pack card shows a 24:00:00 countdown (on its top border); when it reaches zero the pack can no longer be bought, ever, for that player. Also decided for its contents (display shown, no logic yet): + ×5 Lucky Rolls and a 5:00 all-income boost, with 100 gems and 5,000 coins. Open: when the 24 h starts (Claude's pick: the first join after the tutorial, saved per player), and what the income boost multiplies. Building it needs a save field for the start time (roblox-data) and a server check before the purchase prompt and in ProcessReceipt. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Window background colour tries (Play client only)]]

- **2026-11-13 — The 11-12 UI restyle is rebuilt in Edit** (Allen: "Yes, rebuild in Edit", after Play crashed and took the Play-client-only restyle with it). Rebuilt from the session transcript's own Luau calls replayed against StarterGui, so it matches what he approved; the live hooks become window-script code. From now on, long live-tweak sessions get ported to Edit in batches as they go.
- **2026-11-13 — Tab looks follow the lit state in one shared module** (rebuild of the 11-12 restyle): every themed tab is painted by `TabStyle`, one watcher per tab, instead of the stacked preview watchers that fought on Allen's client.
- **2026-11-13 — 11-12's Edit decisions stand; their build is being redone.** The decisions logged on 11-12 (Damascus / Sapphire Epic, lever villager removed, weather line, welcome-back window, Featured, sale chat line) are unchanged; only their Studio build was lost in the crash and is being replayed.
- **2026-11-13 — The AutoRecovery file is the base** (Allen saved it over the Swordsmith place): it held every 11-12 Edit build, so those stand as built; only the Play-only restyle is replayed on top.
- **2026-11-12 — Short of coins or gems: opt-in, no prompt** (Allen: "Yes make it opt-in", after the live data: 616 shortfall prompts, 0 bought): the coin or gem counter's green + pulses three times and the player's own shake or notice shows; tapping the + opens the Shop at Currency. **Supersedes** 2026-10-31's "goes straight to Roblox's purchase prompt". Claude's defaults: no new UI, the pulse only when a covering pack is on sale and after the lessons, one pulse under Reduce motion. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Shortfall offer made opt-in]]
- **2026-11-12 — The pack card** (Allen: "wouldn't it be better if it showed a gui that says you're missing x coins, and then show the closest coin pack that covers the difference", then "Yes, build that"): short of coins or gems shows a small card at the toasts' spot: "Missing 120 coins", the covering pack's icon and amount, and a Buy that opens Roblox's prompt. Not modal: it shrinks away after 4 s, at most once every 2 min (the + pulses in between). It replaces the "Not enough" toast or notice. Builds on the opt-in decision above; brings back a card (removed 2026-10-31) with one Buy and no second step. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Pack card: missing coins and the covering pack]]
- **2026-11-12 — The pack card has no border** (Allen: "i like it with no border actually"): the thin edge art is gone; the card is just its dark gradient with rounded corners. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Pack card: missing coins and the covering pack]]
- **2026-11-12 — The pack card gets a red X** (Allen: "i feel there should also just be an x button on the top right to close it"): top-right, the windows' X at card size; it closes the card before its 4 s are up. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Pack card: missing coins and the covering pack]]
- **2026-11-13 — Restyle rebuilt; Shop has four tabs for good.** The Packs tab stays hidden (Featured covers the Starter row, the bundle and the packs row); the Starter chest breathes instead of wiggling; tab colours and sizes live in `TabStyle`.
- **2026-11-13 — The Starter's crossed-out value is live.** It counts its gems and coins at the best packs' Robux rates plus 9 for the 5 Lucky Rolls (the 11-12 estimate); the "3× VALUE" tag is retired.
- **2026-11-12 — PackOffer hides its card at start** (after Allen: "that window is staying up while im in playtest"): a fix, not a design change; a card template left Visible in Edit can't show in Play again. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Pack card: missing coins and the covering pack]]
- **2026-11-13 — 2x Coins reads "Double your income!"** (Allen), on its Passes card and its Everything Bundle tile; 2x Gems stays "From all earnings".
- **2026-11-13 — The "missing X" pack card shows on every short tap** (Allen: "i want it to happen everytime i click for an upgrade and don't have enough"). It was once per 120 s after live data showed 616 shortfall prompts and 0 buys; the card is not modal (4 s, X to close), so repeats cost a glance. Watch the Offer source in analytics after release.
- **2026-11-12 — Crew role tabs share one warm orange** (Allen: "can we find a better color to fit all these buttons at once", picked "A" from a mockup): replaces a colour per role. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Shop buttons and Crew tabs ported to Edit]]
- **2026-11-12 — Shop buttons take the Upgrades window's Buy look** (Allen: "can we try replacing the shop buy buttons with the buy buttons from upgrades", then the Gift buttons, the Lucky Rolls chips and Odds): black outline, top shine, a darker underside; Gift buttons square and always just left of their Buy. Shop descriptions 20 pt under 26 pt pass titles (Allen: "its basically the same size as the title"), and pass text starts past the icon's badge. Outlines stay as they were (thinner and none were tried and reverted). [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Shop buttons and Crew tabs ported to Edit]]
